## Document 003: 10月FOMC議事要旨：12月利上げ視野、少数ながら50bp議論 ～FRBは2027年のインフレ鈍化シナリオを維持～

Source PDF: all report pdf/10月FOMC議事要旨：12月利上げ視野、少数ながら50bp議論   ～FRBは2027年のインフレ鈍化シナリオを維持～.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-11-19
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

FED  Watching

2026 年 11 月 19 日

1/4 
10 月 FOMC 議事要旨：12 月利上げ視野、少数ながら 50bp 議論 
～FRB は 2027 年のインフレ鈍化シナリオを維持～ 
 経済調査部 主任エコノミスト  桂畑 誠治  
（℡：050-5474-7493）

要旨  
・12 月利上げ再開がメインシナリオ：10 月 27〜28 日開催のFOMC議事要旨が公表された。
同会合では政策金利の据え置きが決定されたものの、参加者のほぼ全員が「今後数カ月間（12 月
15〜16 日会合を含む）での利上げ再開」を基本路線として維持していることが確認された。

・タカ派的テールリスクの浮上：一方で、2〜3 名の参加者がデータ次第で「50bpの大幅利上
げ」を検討する余地に言及しており、市場ではタカ派的なテールリスクが意識される内容となっ
た。

・2027 年のインフレ鈍化見通しは不変：FRBのベースライン（基本見通し）では、インフレは
目先高止まりするものの、関税影響の減退やエネルギー・AI関連の供給制約緩和に伴い、2027
年にかけて鈍化し、時間の経過とともに目標の 2％へ収束していくとの見方は崩れていない。

1．新体制下での情報発信と議事 要旨 の重要性  
新体制移行後、会合直後の声明文は大幅に簡素化され、議長の記者会見も短縮されるなど、直
近の直接的な情報発信は絞り込まれている。その分、後日公表される議事要旨は高い情報量を維
持しており、FRBの本音や委員会内部の議論の分布を推し量る最重要ツールとしての重みが増して
いる。もっとも、公表がFOMC終了から 3 週間後となるため、その間に発表された最新データとの
タイムラグ（情報の遅効性）を考慮して評価する必要がある。

2．10 月会合 の政策 判断 と金融 政策 の基本 スタンス  
10 月会合では、FF レート誘導目標レンジ（3.75％〜4.00％）の据え置きが決定された（投票権
を持つ委員ベースでは 12 名中 10 名が賛成、2 名が反対）。据え置きを支持した委員は、「さらなる
情報の蓄積が、インフレ率を 2％の目標へ適時に戻すための適切な政策スタンスを見極める上で役
立つ」と指摘した。今回の据え置きは利上げサイクルの終了ではなく、累積的な引き締め効果と最
新データを確認するための「一時停止（スキップ）」という位置づけである。

### Page 2

2/4 
今後の政策決定については、「新たに入手されるデータ、変化する見通し、リスクバランスに依存
する」とのデータ次第（データ・ディペンデント）の姿勢を強調した。その上で、基本見通しの下
では「9 月の経済・金利予測（SEP：議長自身は今回も提出せず）とおおむね整合的な形で、今後数
カ月間（12 月 15〜16 日の次回会合など）で追加利上げを行うことが適切となる可能性が高い」と
の見解で、参加者のほぼ全員が一致した。

3．経済・物価・雇用の現状認識と 中長期 の見通し  
委員会全体の共通認識を確認すると、景気については「底堅い家計支出と堅調な設備投資に支え
られ、経済活動は引き続き力強いペースで拡大している」との見方で一致した。 
労働市場については「安定しており、最大雇用に近づいている」と判断され、目先も安定した状
態が続き、雇用の上振れリスクと下振れリスクはおおむね均衡していると評価された。 
インフレに関しては、依然として高止まりしており、物価上昇圧力は経済全体に広範に及んでい
るとの指摘が相次いだ。特に高いエネルギー価格や精製燃料の供給制約が足元の押し上げ要因とな
っており、見通しには上振れリスクが意識されている。一方、名目賃金の伸びは緩やかな水準にと
どまり、2％のインフレ目標とおおむね整合的と判断された。 
中長期のインフレ見通しでは、9 月コア CPI（前月比＋0.3％）の上振れを受けても、スタッフの
インフレ予測は 9 月会合時点からほとんど変更されず、参加者の間でも従来の基本見通しが維持さ
れた。大半の参加者は、インフレ率は目先高止まりするものの、関税引き上げに伴う価格転嫁圧力
の減退や、エネルギー価格・AI 関連投資に伴う供給制約（電力・インフラ需要等）の緩和により、
時間の経過とともに（9 月 SEP で示された 2027 年の鈍化軌道に沿って）2％に向けて低下していく
と予想した。中長期的なインフレ期待も引き続き 2％と整合的であり、これが過度な利上げ回数の
積み増し（ターミナルレートの大幅引き上げ）を抑制する根拠となっている。

4．タカ派サプライズ：「 12 月 50bp 利上げ」への言及と今後の焦点  
今回の議事要旨における最大の注目点（タカ派サプライズ）は、9 月 SEP に沿った追加利上げ（25bp
ずつ）というメインシナリオの裏で、大幅利上げの選択肢が議論されていたことである。「新たに入
手されるインフレデータが上振れれば、次回 12 月会合で 25bp を超える利上げ（50bp など）が検討
に値する」と発言した参加者が 2〜3 名いたことが明らかとなった。 
議事要旨の公表前にあたる11月10日に発表された10月コアCPIは前月比＋0.3％と3カ月連続
で市場予想を上回り、3 カ月年率換算ペースでは目標を大幅に上回る高い伸びとなった。さらに、
10 月会合では据え置きに賛成したウォラーFRB 理事も、10 月 CPI の結果を受けて「50bp の利上げ
を検討すべき」との趣旨の発言を行っており、同理事は議事要旨内の「タカ派の少数意見」に含ま
れていたとみられる。

5．マーケットへのインプリケーション  
現時点では 50bp 利上げは少数意見にとどまるが、FRB が「インフレ再燃リスクに対して後手に回

場の織り込みが「25bp 利上げ」から「50bp 利上げ」へと急速に傾くリスクがある。債券市場ではタ
ーミナルレートの上振れを意識する形で米 2 年債利回りに上昇圧力がかかりやすく、為替市場でも
ドル高・円安地合いが継続しやすい展開が見込まれる。

## Document 099: 2027年美股行情展望：回归均衡，谋定而后动

回归均衡，谋定而后动 
报告导读： 
我们的观点：2027 年美股或延续内部结构再平衡，非AI 资产广度扩散有望支撑指数韧性，结构性波动仍大于指数整体波动。
AI 和通胀构成两大核心矛盾：AI 产业景气度定全年增长斜率，通胀&利率定全年交易节奏

我们的逻辑：增长斜率和流动性的时间之争：（1）分子端-2027 年美股盈利增长动能或边际放缓。结构层面：2027 年美国
AI 与非 AI 板块间的 K 型分化有望收敛，周期和科技对于美股盈利增长的贡献趋于降速，盈利动能贡献有望更均衡。（2）
分母端：宏观流动性先紧后松，AI 信用局部风险暴露。

1.  2026 年海外权益行情回顾：市场从极致 K 型分化走向结构性再平衡 
1.1   “AI 抱团-瓦解-再平衡”行情极致演绎：26 年美股更多为结构性波动，指数总体具备韧性 
回顾2026 年，美股经历了“AI 抱团-瓦解-再平衡”行情的极致演绎，概括而言，美股指数韧性强于个股、
结构波动大于总量波动。上半年，地缘冲突压制宏观风险偏好，AI 产业景气构成基本面狭窄的繁荣，市场缩圈
至业绩确定性最强的AI 硬件资产。下半年，AI 下游商业化增长动能边际放缓叠加市场对CSP 资本开支可持续
性及投资回报的质疑，前期极致抱团的AI 行情逐步瓦解，拥挤的筹码结构放大了波动。与此同时，若排除地
缘扰动的外生影响，非AI 资产亦能展现较强的韧性，实现更广泛的行业扩散，一定程度对冲了AI 板块调整的
负面影响，使得指数整体波动相对收敛。 
图 1：26 年美股更多为结构性波动，指数总体具备韧性  图 2：上半年 AI 资产贡献标普 500 上涨主要驱动，下
半年非 AI 资产再平衡对冲科技下跌对于指数的拖累

数上涨驱动）

资料来源：彭博，国泰君安期货研究

1.2  行情核心驱动：AI 产业景气趋势主导方向，抱团交易瓦解放大波动 
2026 上半年：AI 产业景气突破成为核心驱动，盈利增长消化估值压力。上半年，尽管地缘冲突扰动持续
压制宏观风险偏好，但AI Agent（Anthropic）应用场景取得突破性进展进一步强化AI 产业稀缺的景气度，
较强的盈利增长对前期估值形成消化和支撑，驱动AI 板块上涨。

2026 下半年：AI 产业潜在风险逐步显现，市场对于AI 产业叙事的预期开始松动，杀估值构成行情调整
的核心驱动，此外，资金面微观结构恶化放大波动。2026Q3 以来，市场基本完成了对于“AI 二阶导（增速）”
反转的确认，而对于“AI 一阶导（增长）”反转更多停留在预期层面质疑的加深而非现实端的确认。因此，从
DDM 拆解来看，下半年全球科技股调整更多表现为杀估值而非杀业绩。

2.  分子端：增长由高波动转向温和收敛，盈利增长降速，K 型分化收敛

总量层面：2027 年美股盈利增长动能或边际放缓。结构层面：2027 年美国AI 与非AI 板块间的 K 型分化
有望收敛，周期和科技对于美股盈利增长的贡献趋于降速，盈利动能贡献有望更均衡。具体而言：（1）科技板
块：高基数效应下，若下游商业化兑现不及预期或进展偏慢，AI 算力链周期属性强化，2027 年美股科技板块
盈利增长降速是中性假设；（ 2）能源板块：地缘和油价是核心变量，高基数效应下2027 年盈利增长趋于降
速；（3）传统板块：财政脉冲由支撑转为温和拖累，退税规模较2026 年的高位回落，房地产、传统资本开支
与低收入消费修复节奏更多取决于通胀和宏观流动性。

2.1  宏观：增长由高波动转向温和收敛 
增长由高波动转向温和收敛，通胀有望趋势回落，K 型分化收敛。2027 年美国实际GDP 季环比折年率四个
季度分别预计为2.0%、2.1%、2.1%和2.2%，全年均值约2.1%，较2026 年的2.5%回落0.4 个百分点；但季
度间振幅由2026 年的3.2 个百分点降至0.2 个百分点。2026 年的高波动源于政府停摆后的基数扰动、关税与
能源价格冲击以及AI 投资的一次性脉冲，这些因素在2027 年基本淡出，增长回到2%上下的潜在增速附近。
以四季度同比衡量，2027 年预期为2.1%，较2026 年同期的2.3%小幅回落0.2 个百分点，全年同比在2.0%—
2.3%之间，呈"中枢微降、路径平坦"的形态。驱动来看，财政脉冲由支撑转为温和拖累，退税规模较2026 年
的高位回落；房地产、传统资本开支与低收入消费受高利率压制更为明显；AI 相关投资仍在贡献增量，但边际
增速回落。 
图 13：美国主要宏观经济指标展望及美联储 9 月 SEP 展望（26Q4 后为预期值）

资料来源：国泰君安期货研究

2.1.1  中期选举后政经背景变化及对市场的影响 
展望前方，经济议题将是2027 年乃至2028 年大选年中最关键的政治博弈战场，“降低通胀”和“弥合经
济K 分化”将是持续性的政治诉求——分别对应着降低油价的天然诉求和对AI 板块的监管、再分配诉求。 
本次中期选举点出美国政经气候变化最大的特征，是选民焦点已经从意识形态的“左右”之争转为经济阶
层的“上下”之争、从文化之争转为经济之争，“K 型分化”最终清晰的显示在投票版图中，成为民主党的
Tailwind。在“由红转蓝”的部分关键摇摆州，民主党候选人以一种对“意识形态”分歧较为克制和中立的态
度，但火力全开直指降低物价等经济诉求。在过去数十年的选举周期中，“左右”之争中如移民问题、少数群
体、宗教、“美国至上”（MAGA）、去精英化等议题，在当前高物价、K 型分化、贫富分化等经济失衡加剧的背景
下再度煽动意识形态武器已然不合时宜，而共和党候选人在应对经济议题时显然无法与特朗普政府进行切割。
事实上，民主党正在获得部分MAGA 选票，2024 共和党的MAGA 群体与2026 民主党的劳动人民群体在经济逻辑
上并没有那么大差异。蓝色狂潮必然会阻碍科技和AI 进展？我们不这么认为，基本面逻辑而非政经变化仍旧
是我们前期相对看空科技板块的主因，选举只是阶段性强化这一方向。但是必须要看到“Technocracy”或者
“科技寡头-政治”的联盟已经成为当前经济背景下的对立面。

### Page 8

请务必阅读正文之后的免责条款部分 
 8 
图 14：2026 年美国中期选举选民议题关注度排序

资料来源：国泰君安期货研究

落到对金融市场影响看，美国政经变化如何影响中短期市场，哪些领域政策变化概率较大、哪些领域实质
性影响不大？——我们从三类路径看可为和不可为。一是不依赖新立法、总统可自行决定：如232、301 关税
已在实际执行，对华301 条款10 月7 日确认继续有效；先进芯片出口许可与对外投资审查已有法律基础（《综
合对外投资国家安全法》）、军事行动属总统权限——无太大变化，国会能做的只有拨款附加条件、听证和诉
讼，属于降低行政执行效率的施压手段。二是需要新立法+参议院60 票+总统签署：如企业税率从21%上调至
25%—28%、恢复ACA 增强补贴与联邦医保补贴、恢复被削减的IRA 清洁能源税惠、收回总统关税授权等——民
主党推行自己的议程并非易事，对当前市场主线影响不大。普通法案在参议院需60 票才能结束辩论，推翻否
决需两院各三分之二多数。三是必须通过的法案与参议院专属权力：如12 月11 日到期的临时拨款（覆盖全部
12 项年度拨款）、已在众议院以216:212 通过的2027 财年国防授权法、延至12 月11 日的《国防生产法》授
权、2027 年债务上限；以及参议院人事确认权（美联储理事、联邦法官、内阁）与众议院传票、监督权等。—
—重要财政变化和显著债务扩张难度仍大，但我们会更关注年底债务上限博弈和政府停摆风险。

图 15：参议院多数立法需 60 票（预算协调除外），且总统可否决，因此民主党掌控一院或两院，主要作用是阻
止共和党议程，而非推行自己的议程

2.1.2  地缘-油价-通胀情景如何演变？通胀依赖同比基数效应

我们认为2027 年美国通胀趋势有所回落、中枢下降。但通胀趋势依赖同比基数，且近端2026Q4-2027Q1
通胀读数仍旧较高，2027Q2 同比基数效应显现。 
当前中东地缘形式总体看既保留谈判通道但局势也未实质性降温。2026 年2 月底美伊冲突爆发以来，霍
尔木兹海峡通行受伊朗方面制约，成为双方博弈的核心筹码。美国中期选举和以色列大选关键转折，美国中期
选举中民主党夺回参众两院，以色列方面内塔尼亚胡阵营失去议会多数，两者强化了冲突降温预期。外交层
面，卡塔尔、巴基斯坦持续斡旋，伊朗提出以美方解除制裁、解冻约80 亿美元资产为条件、两天内重开海峡
的“两天计划”，伊朗扬称最高领袖已下令结束“非战非和”状态、愿以谈判破局。但实质进展尚未落地，伊朗
同时表示并未与美国直接谈判，11 月以来美军对伊朗仍有“小型打击”；伊朗革命卫队解除导弹射程限制、美
军向阿曼湾货船开火；海峡接连发生油轮触雷起火与爆炸事件，沙特与胡塞武装的互袭亦未停止。 
对于原油市场而言，我们的观点是“去繁存简”，中长期仍旧视作宏观风险，一方面，主要因地缘核心矛盾
未解决，使得供给持续回归具有脆弱性（伊朗仍有袭击海峡油轮的能力、胡塞武装仍有干扰沙特原油供应能
力），原油价格持续蕴含地缘溢价，低库存水平亦支持原油估值。但另一方面，中东供给流量逐步向战前水平
恢复、运费价格有所回落、下游需求对高油价负反馈、高油价高通胀下的政治压力等也真实抑制油价上行空
间。我们按照原油75-110 美元/桶宽幅区间震荡，来对2027 年通胀水平和趋势做测算。 
我们大致测算，油价每变动10 美元/桶，2027 年CPI 同比约变动0.35 个百分点。基准情形下：75 美元
对应约1.97%，85 美元对应约2.32%，95 美元对应约2.68%，100 美元对应约2.85%，110 美元对应约
3.21%。不过，此模型仅仅考虑“油价-汽油-通胀权重-通胀”的传导路径，未能考虑1）战略储备释放、燃油
税调整等政策对冲；2）成品油裂解价差大幅走阔风险带来更强的通胀传导；3）工资—价格螺旋与通胀预期；
4）下游需求负反馈程度。 
另外，归总市场一致预期、彭博经济学家预测、市场定价和BE Model 四方预测美国CPI 同比在2026Q4-
2027Q1 维持在3.0-3.8%区间，在2027Q2 回落至2.1-2.9%区间，在2027Q3-2027Q4 回落至1.9-2.6%区

2.2  科技：年内估值下杀已基本消化 AI 产业降速的预期，2027 年更多面临现实端盈利的考验

自2025Q4 开始，“AI 交易”的风险点逐步浮出，我们大致构建了对AI 产业风险定位的框架：①一阶段
（杀估值）是在AI 下游商业化“增速”见顶放缓后对“AI 二阶导”反转的确认（对应6-8 月全球科技板块调整
的底层逻辑）；②二阶段是在中游CSP 投资回报率回落&自由现金流转负&债务融资信用风险攀升背景下对于远
期Capex 持续性预期层面质疑的加深（对应9 月以来海外股债背离加深的底层逻辑）； ③三阶段（杀业绩）是
在上游硬件价格松动、上游营收和盈利“增长”见顶回落后对于“AI 一阶导”反转的确认（尚未出现，2027
年可能逐步确认）。

展望2027 年，AI 交易的风险点随时间累积：两种情景—AI 下游商业化场景进一步兑现 OR 海外股债非对
称定价逐渐收敛。2026Q3 以来，市场基本完成了对于“AI 二阶导（增速）”反转的确认，而对于“AI 一阶导
（增长）”反转更多停留在预期层面质疑的加深而非现实端的确认。因此，从DDM 拆解来看，下半年全球科技
股调整更多表现为杀估值而非杀业绩。展望2027 年，两种情景—AI 下游商业化场景进一步兑现 OR 股债非对
称定价逐渐收敛：（ 1）乐观情景下，AI 下游实现更广义的商业化兑现（AI Agent&物理AI）—>AI 投资链实现
闭环（估值修复，业绩增长）；（2）悲观情景下，逐渐确认“AI 一阶导”反转（Capex 下修/上游硬件价格崩塌）
——>市场担忧从AI 产业增速见顶（杀估值）—>AI 产业增长见顶（杀业绩）。 
图 18：股债定价背离定位 AI 投资周期进入苛刻的下半场：25Q4 以来海外信用市场率先定价 Capex 融资持
续性与回报约束的风险，而海外权益市场定价明显隐含非对称风险

资料来源：彭博，国泰君安期货研究

### Page 12

请务必阅读正文之后的免责条款部分 
 12 
图 19：2026Q3 以来：市场基本完成了对于“AI 二阶导”反转的确认，而对于“AI 一阶导”反转更多停留
在预期层面质疑的加深而非现实端的确认

资料来源：彭博，国泰君安期货研究

2.2.1  CSP“ROIC-WACC”临界点渐近？AI 下游兑现与上游投资之间的“时差”是当前产业核心瑕疵 
北美云计算厂商ROIC-WACC 的临界点持续逼近：过去一年，北美 CSP 的ROIC-WACC 持续收敛，
Neocloud 的ROIC-WACC 几乎已全线倒挂。 
AI 上游 Capex 维持高强度&下游兑现放缓（ROIC↓）： 核心矛盾在于AI 下游需求兑现速度尚未充分匹配上
游基础设施投资扩张速度（26Q3 Anthropic ARR 放缓进一步激化矛盾）；另一方面，AI 投资下半场，前期巨
额Capex 投入对于利润的侵蚀正在逐步显性化，资产负债表对于下游需求兑现的容错率降低。 
AI 产业信用溢价走廓&宏观利率高企（WACC↑）： 一方面，信用利差走廓反映信用市场对于AI 投资风险的
重新定价：Capex 持续性与回报约束的焦虑加深&供给端恶化（科技企业大规模发债）；另一方面，地缘反复抬
高油价&通胀&海外宏观利率中枢亦间接抬升科技企业融资成本中枢。

### Page 13

请务必阅读正文之后的免责条款部分 
 13

观利率高企（WACC↑）

2.2.2  Capex 持续性的质疑是否会从预期走向现实？CSP 现金流的约束在显性化，对下游兑现容错率降低 
去年Q4 以来海外股债定价开始背离，今年9 月以来海外股债定价背离加深的底层逻辑：市场对AI 资本开
支可持续性的担忧，正在从对未来盈利与投资回报的预期博弈逐步转向对现实融资约束和资产负债表承载能力
的审视。截至2026Q3，北美 CSP 延续高强度 AI 资本开支，除微软外自由现金流均转负（需依靠外部融资维
持Capex），CSP 现金流约束正在逐步显性化，意味着后续对下游兑现容错率进一步降低。 
图 29：北美 CSP 延续高强度 AI 资本开支，除微软外自由现金流均转负（需依靠外部融资维持 Capex）

资料来源：彭博，国泰君安期货研究

2.2.3  Cycle 还是Super Cycle？若下游需求放缓难以证伪，AI 基建定价体系回归周期属性 
若AI 下游商业化放缓、AI 上游投资回报承压等风险持续累积且难以证伪，市场对AI 基础设施的定价逻
辑或将从超级周期（Super Cycle）逐步回归传统资本开支周期（Cycle）：2027 年AI 基建增长降速是中性假
设。历史经验，北美CSP 及费城半导体估值下修往往领先EPS 增速下修1-3 季度。 
自2025Q4 开始，“AI 交易”的风险点逐步浮出，我们大致构建了对AI 产业风险定位的框架：①一阶段
（杀估值）是在AI 下游商业化“增速”见顶放缓后对“AI 二阶导”反转的确认（对应6-8 月全球科技板块调整
的底层逻辑）；②二阶段是在中游CSP 投资回报率回落&自由现金流转负&债务融资信用风险攀升背景下对于远
期Capex 持续性预期层面质疑的加深（对应9 月以来海外股债背离加深的底层逻辑）； ③三阶段（杀业绩）是
在上游硬件价格松动、上游营收和盈利“增长”见顶回落后对于“AI 一阶导”反转的确认（尚未出现）。关
注：（1）GPU 租赁价格& HBM/DRAM 合约及现货价格；（ 2）头部LLM ARR 环比增速；（3）GPU 及服务器订单砍
单/延迟提货风险；（4）AI 融资链金融违约风险（主体评级、CDS 利差、IPO 认购倍数）；（5）Capex 预期是否
下修等维度。

2.2.4  蓝色浪潮对于 AI 产业实质性影响几何？供给端因素更多影响节奏，需求端兑现决定产业方向

蓝色浪潮一定程度强化了地方层面对AI 基础设施投资建设的政策约束。随着民主党在地方选举中取得进
展，部分州及地方政府可能加强对数据中心建设的监管，重点涉及电力消耗、环保标准、土地使用、税收优惠
及公用事业成本等方面，因此可能推高数据中心建设及运营成本，延长项目审批周期，并对新增产能的落地节
奏形成约束。 
整体而言，政策约束更多作用于AI 基础设施的供给侧，可能影响资本开支的落地节奏、建设成本及区域
布局，而AI 下游需求端兑现最终决定产业方向。一方面，相关限制措施因州而异，且多数重大政策调整仍需
经过立法、监管及行政审批程序，实际落地存在一定时滞；另一方面，已经开工或完成前期审批的项目短期受
到的影响相对有限，CSP 也可以通过调整选址、迁移项目及增加能源配套投资等方式应对。

3.  分母端：宏观流动性先紧后松，AI 信用局部风险暴露

3.1  宏观流动性：我们总体判断起点偏紧，全年先紧后松 
2027 年宏观流动性对整体宏观情绪定调有重要影响，我们总体判断起点偏紧，全年先紧后松。当前美国
金融条件已处于边际收紧态势，主要来自利率上行、美元指数走强、信用利差走阔和美股下行多种原因共振。 
美联储预计在12 月和明年3 月各加息25bp 至4.25%-4.50%，此后按兵不动。也有观点认为降息最早在
2027 年11 月启动。因此，全年大致是上半年维持偏紧、下半年视利率回落程度、股票市场和信用市场以及美
元指数从“危机模式”中走出，带动整体金融条件指数逐步转松。 
分项看2027 年宏观流动性： 
一方面看货币政策，上半年仍旧处在升息曲线中，对宏观流动性有所限制，三季度触峰回落对宏观流动性
边际贡献宽松，对加息结束的定价或在通胀显著回落或油价明显下行的可能性下重新开启。当前市场预计
2026 年12 月会议加息约1 次，此后加息预期继续累积，2027 年1 月底加息一次，隐含利率约4.27%；至3
月再度加息，隐含利率升至4.4%以上。加息定价在二季度明显放缓，2027 年7 月至9 月前后见顶，峰值隐含
利率约4.55%，相当于较当前再上行约65bp 左右。2027 三季度后，曲线小幅回落，至2027 年11 月至12 月
隐含利率回落至4.45%左右。这说明市场预期本轮加息在2027 年年中结束，下半年开始计入小幅降息，但全
年政策利率仍明显高于当前水平。 
另一方面看金融条件如股票市场、信用市场和美元指数，整体上看，当前我们认为股票市场调整在中期尚
未结束，而信用市场尤其是科技板块自由现金流恶化预计延续至2027 年中，信用利差或对金融条件指数构成
拖累，美元指数跟随利差逻辑和市场风偏较难判断，但预计美元指数在2027Q1 维持相对强势，对金融条件构
成抑制，而后回落对边际改善金融条件。

3.2  信用风险：AI 产业融资循环链条局部信用风险值得动态关注 
3.2.1  AI 基建投资正在明显增加产业链相关公司对债务融资的依赖 
据IMF 数据：北美云服务商在全球投资级债券发行中的份额从2024 年的不足1%升至2026 年的约10%。
虽然其核心业务盈利强劲，但持续高额的Capex 投资使得FCF 快速下降（除微软外均已转负），AI 基建投资正
在明显增加对债务融资的依赖。而Neocloud 等企业通常负债更高、盈利能力更弱，可能成为AI 生态体系中的
薄弱环节。

 22

3.2.2  AI 循环融资系统（Circular Financing）加大了债务、股权和未来收入之间的相关性

据《Urbanomics ：Update on the AI spending boom》分析：AI 融资体系可能存在三类相互重叠的
风险——循环融资（Circular Financing）、供应商融资（Vendor Financing）和表外杠杆（Off-
balance-sheet Leverage）。它们并非相互独立的融资交易，而是横跨七类产业参与者、相互关联的信用风
险结构。 
从融资机制看，AI 产业正在形成资金循环，使得原本相互独立的双边交易，演变为具有自我强化机制的融
资体系。芯片厂商及Hyperscalers 向AI Lab 提供股权投资，AI Lab 随后将获得的资金转化为对芯片厂商和
云服务商的采购承诺，这些采购承诺进一步形成供应商的在手订单（Backlog）并支撑其债券融资；与此同
时，金融机构通过SPV 和私人信贷为整个AI 基础设施建设提供资金。 
AI 融资体系可能存在三类相互重叠的风险：（1）AI 循环融资可能放大表观需求。（2）关键主体的风险可
能沿AI 产业链同步传导，原本分散在不同环节的风险实际上依赖于同一底层需求假设。如果OpenAI 无法履行
相关承诺，Oracle/CoreWeave 的在手订单、NVIDIA 与AMD 的销售预期，以及SPV 贷款人的抵押品价值，都
可能同时受到重新定价。（3）风险可能向透明度较低的融资渠道转移（私人信贷和SPV 等结构），近两年私人
信贷市场的杠杆与流动性风险持续上升。 
总体而言，只有当AI 需求不及预期时，相关风险才可能集中显现，但由于七类参与者实际上共同承担对
AI 需求增长的押注，一旦需求明显放缓，相关风险可能在多个环节同步暴露，潜在冲击可能通过企业、金融机
构及居民资产负债表向金融市场和实体经济扩散。 
图 49：AI 循环融资（Circular Financing）的七类交易主体

3.2.3  2027 年尚未出现全面信用收缩的典型特征，但AI 链条局部信用风险值得动态关注

展望2027 年：美国尚未出现全面信用收缩的典型特征，但AI 链条局部信用及流动性脆弱性有所累积。当
前，美国银行体系资本充足率仍接近历史较高水平，企业与居民债务占GDP 比重持续回落，整体债务脆弱性及
信用风险相对可控，尚未出现全面信用收缩的典型特征，但AI 产业链条循环融资体系内结构性风险值得关
注。

4.  美股结论与投资展望：增配价值，回归均衡，谋定而后动

市场判断：2027 年美股或延续内部结构再平衡，非AI 资产广度扩散有望支撑指数韧性，结构性波动仍大
于指数整体波动，AI 产业景气度定全年增长弹性，通胀&利率定全年交易节奏。 
战略层面：增配价值与防御，回归均衡配置。我们去年以来持续提示的AI 产业四阶段框架，2027 年大致
处于第二阶段向第三阶段逐步过渡的窗口：建议降低AI 硬件，增配必选消费、医疗保健、公用事业等低AI 相
关性资产，回归盈利质量与现金流稳定性增强组合韧性。更长期的视角（第四阶段）， AI 下游（CSP/应用/软
件/物理AI 等）将成为主线资产（目前仍然处于左侧观察区间）。 
战术层面：顺势博弈通胀缓和与利率回落驱动的修复行情。若通胀改善、利率中枢下行且经济保持韧性，
金融条件改善有望催化房地产、可选消费等传统型利率敏感资产+医疗保健代表的成长型利率敏感资产的阶段性
反弹。

图 55：战略层面：2027 年建议战略增配价值防御资产，组合配置回归均衡

资料来源：国泰君安期货研究

2317 鴻海精密工業(FOXCONN)

壹、訪 談／法 說 內 容 
重點摘要： 
 3Q26 營收 3.02 兆，YoY +46% ，毛利率 6.02%，EPS 5.51
元。單季營收、營業利益、稅後淨利均創下歷年單季新高。 
 受惠於 AI 營運持續成長及 ICT 產品進入傳統出貨旺季，公司
預期 4Q26 整體營收季對季與年對年均將呈現強勁成長。AI 伺
服器部分，由於新一代機櫃市佔率較上一代提升，預期第四季
AI 機櫃出貨量將維持高雙位數季增。 
 訂單能見度轉佳，公司上修 2026 年全年四大產品展望，對 2027
年全年營運持審慎樂觀看法，預計整體營收維持雙位數成長，
AI 機櫃則預估明年出貨維持高雙位數成長。

一. 3Q26 營收、獲利續創單季新高，EPS 5.51 元 
鴻海 3Q26 營收 3.02 兆，QoQ +19% ，YoY +46% ，續創歷史新
高，主要受惠於 AI 伺服器呈現強勁成長，其中，第三季 ASIC AI
伺服器出貨量年增 1.5 倍，高於 GPU 約 70%的成長率，帶動整體
AI 機櫃達成高雙位數成長目標，且新一代 AI 機櫃已開始出貨。就
各產品別來看，3Q26 雲端網路產品佔整體營收的 54%；消費智
能產品佔 29%；電腦終端產品佔 12%；元件及其他產品佔 5%，
而獲利方面，3Q26 營業毛利 1815.1 億，QoQ +17%，YoY +39%，
毛利率 6.02%，QoQ -0.1ppts，YoY -0.33ppts，營業利益 1153.5
億，QoQ +22% ，YoY+64%，營利率 3.83%，QoQ +0.07ppts ，
YoY +0.4ppts，3Q26 業外淨利 33.6 億，稅後淨利 775.2 億，QoQ 
+29%，YoY +34%，單季稅後 EPS 5.51 元，創歷年單季新高。

二. AI 機櫃放量、ICT 產品進入旺季，4Q26 營運有望持
續成長 
公司公告 10 月營收 1 兆 3053 億，MoM +12.7%，YoY +45.7%，
今年累計營收 8.97 兆，YoY +40.4% 。展望 4Q26，受惠於 AI 營
運持續成長及 ICT 產品進入傳統出貨旺季，公司預期整體營收季
對季與年對年均將呈現強勁成長。其中，AI 伺服器部分，由於新
一代機櫃市佔率較上一代提升，預期第四季 AI 機櫃出貨量將維持
高雙位數季增。而傳統 ICT 產品，消費智能產品在旺季需求與 ASP
提升下，預期將呈現強勁表現；電腦終端產品則受限於記憶體等
零組件供應吃緊影響，預期將略微季減。預估公司 4Q26 營收 3.59
兆，QoQ +18.7%，YoY +37.9%，毛利率 5.8%，營利率 3.5%，
稅後淨利 811 億元，單季稅後 EPS 5.77 元。

三. 預估 2026/2027 年 EPS 19.08/23.59 元 
展望 2026 年，由於目前訂單能見度較 8 月更佳，公司上調 2026
年全年四大產品展望，整體營運維持強勁成長。預估 2026 全年營
收 11.26 兆，YoY +39%，毛利率 6.0%，營利率 3.7%，稅後淨利
2685 億，全年稅後 EPS 19.08 元。展望 2027 年，公司對明年全
年營運持審慎樂觀看法，預計維持雙位數成長，其中 AI 仍為最主
要的成長動能。受惠於全球算力需求與基礎設施投資，全球主要
CSP 明年持續擴大 AI 資本支出，預期公司雲網產品佔比將在未來
3 到 5 年，從目前的五成以上進一步提高至 60%以上。而 AI 機櫃
部分，預期 2027 年 AI 機櫃出貨維持高雙位數成長，且客戶除了
大型 CSP 客戶之外，明年將加入更多 NeoCloud 客戶，市佔率將
進一步提升。此外，隨 AI 推論需求增加帶動 ASIC 伺服器快速成
長，其高度客製化需求亦有利公司發揮零組件垂直整合優勢以支
撐獲利表現。消費智能產品由於主要客戶產品以高階為主，受記
憶體等供應鏈短缺影響相對有限，再加上新產品開發與規格升級
需求，預計 2027 年整體表現相對穩定。而電腦終端產品，由於記
憶體供應緊張與零組件價格上漲，可能推升終端售價並影響消費
者換機意願，對明年 PC 市場看法較為審慎。預估 2027 全年營收
14.9 兆，YoY +33%，毛 利率 5.4%，營利率 3.3%，稅後淨利 3320
億，全年稅後 EPS 23.59 元。

四. 投資建議為「買進」，目標價 330 元 
考量到 CSP 持續上修 2027 年資本支出展望，AI 伺服器維持強勁
需求，隨著新一代 AI 機櫃推出，有望進一步拓展公司在 AI 伺服
器領域市佔率，帶動公司營收、獲利持續成長。目前公司股價交
易於 2027 年 EPS 約 10~11 倍本益比，評價仍有提升空間，給予
投資建議為買進，目標價 330 元(14x 2027 EPS)。

貳、產 業 及 營 運 概 況

一.公司簡介 
鴻海精密工業(FOXCONN)為全球最大 eCMMS(電子化-零元件、
模組機光電垂直整合服務)廠，在電子代工服務領域(EMS)排名全
球第一，市占率超過四成。鴻海近年積極投入「電動車、數位健
康、機器人」三大新興產業以及「人工智慧、半導體、新世代通
訊技術」三項新技術領域，以「3+3」結合作為集團重要的長期發
展策略。在 EMS 板塊，鴻海主要競爭對手為和碩聯合科技、廣達
電腦、緯創資通、仁寶電腦、英業達及立訊精密等。

二.產業趨勢概況 
1. 明年 CSP Capex 預估破 1.5 兆美元，AI 伺服器產值年增逾 9 成 
根據集邦科技(TrendForce)最新研究，受惠於 Google、Amazon、
Meta、Microsoft、Oracle 及中國四大科技巨頭等全球前九大雲端
服務供應商(CSP)擴大投資 AI 基礎設施，預估 2026 年九大 CSP
資本支出總額將逾 9,570 億美元，YoY +106%，2027 年更因伺服
器整機漲價、零組件缺貨漲價及供應商要求預付款等因素，有望
突破 1.5 兆美元，YoY +60%，雙雙優於預期。在超大型 CSP、Tier-2
資料中心與主權 AI 等需求帶動下，預估 2026 年 AI 伺服器產值約
4,790 億美元，占整體伺服器產值近 75%；2027 年隨輝達 GB300、
VR200 與超微 Helios 等整櫃式方案成為主流，加上記憶體等關鍵
零組件短缺推升單價，預計整體伺服器產值將首度跨越 1 兆美元，
其中 AI 伺服器產值將躍升至近 9,200 億美元 YoY +90%，占比進
一步提高至 81%左右。

圖二、2024-2027 年九大 CSP 資本支出總額預估(單位:十億美元)

2. 2026 年全球 AI 伺服器出貨量預計年增 31% 
根據 TrendForce 最新 AI server 產業研究，近期超大型 CSP、
Tier-2 資料中心業者對 NVIDIA GB/VR 等整櫃式 AI server 方案的
採購意願明顯提高，且 Google、AWS 新一代 ASIC 平台於 2026
下半年陸續放量，加上中系業者擴大採用本地 AI 方案以滿足雲端
大型語言模型(LLM) AI 需求，促使 TrendForce 上調 2026 年 AI 
server 出貨年成長幅度，從原先的 28%更新為近 31%。北美五大
業者 2026 年的資本支出多翻倍提升，反映出大型 CSP 廠持續擴
建 AI 資料中心、GPU 叢集、液冷散熱等新一代基礎設施，以應對
生成式 AI 及大型模型快速成長帶來的算力需求。其中，Google 
2026 至 2027 年都將側重自家 TPU 晶片發展，2027 年出貨量有
望繼續高速成長。AWS 2026 年以 NVIDIA GB300 作為 GPU AI 
server 主力，自研 ASIC 也穩定出貨，預期至 2027 年量能將再擴
大。Meta 2026 年主要採用 NVIDIA GB/VR、AMD Helios 整櫃式
方案，自研 ASIC 布局預計至 2027 年將明顯加速，為該公司長期
降低 AI 基礎設施成本、提升推論效率的重要策略。

叁、業 績 及 獲 利 預 估

一. AI 機櫃放量、ICT 產品進入旺季，4Q26 營運有望持
續成長 
公司公告 10 月營收 1 兆 3053 億，MoM +12.7%，YoY +45.7%，
今年累計營收 8.97 兆，YoY +40.4% 。展望 4Q26，受惠於 AI 營
運持續成長及 ICT 產品進入傳統出貨旺季，公司預期整體營收季
對季與年對年均將呈現強勁成長。其中，AI 伺服器部分，由於新
一代機櫃市佔率較上一代提升，預期第四季 AI 機櫃出貨量將維持
高雙位數季增。而傳統 ICT 產品，消費智能產品在旺季需求與 ASP

稅後淨利 811 億元，單季稅後 EPS 5.77 元。

二. 預估 2026/2027 年 EPS 19.08/23.59 元 
展望 2026 年，由於目前訂單能見度較 8 月更佳，公司上調 2026
年全年四大產品展望，整體營運維持強勁成長。預估 2026 全年營
收 11.26 兆，YoY +39%，毛利率 6.0%，營利率 3.7%，稅後淨利
2685 億，全年稅後 EPS 19.08 元。展望 2027 年，公司對明年全
年營運持審慎樂觀看法，預計維持雙位數成長，其中 AI 仍為最主
要的成長動能。受惠於全球算力需求與基礎設施投資，全球主要
CSP 明年持續擴大 AI 資本支出，預期公司雲網產品佔比將在未來
3 到 5 年，從目前的五成以上進一步提高至 60%以上。而 AI 機櫃
部分，預期 2027 年 AI 機櫃出貨維持高雙位數成長，且客戶除了
大型 CSP 客戶之外，明年將加入更多 NeoCloud 客戶，市佔率將
進一步提升。此外，隨 AI 推論需求增加帶動 ASIC 伺服器快速成
長，其高度客製化需求亦有利公司發揮零組件垂直整合優勢以支
撐獲利表現。消費智能產品由於主要客戶產品以高階為主，受記
憶體等供應鏈短缺影響相對有限，再加上新產品開發與規格升級
需求，預計 2027 年整體表現相對穩定。而電腦終端產品，由於記
憶體供應緊張與零組件價格上漲，可能推升終端售價並影響消費
者換機意願，對明年 PC 市場看法較為審慎。預估 2027 全年營收
14.9 兆，YoY +33%，毛 利率 5.4%，營利率 3.3%，稅後淨利 3320
億，全年稅後 EPS 23.59 元。

## Document 133: 261117_daiwa_merida

Source PDF: all report pdf/261117_daiwa_merida.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-17
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Summary: Merida hosted its 3Q26 investor call on 17 November after market hours, 
following release of its 3Q26 results on 11 November (see our memo). Merida’s gross 
margin came in at 15.2% (vs. 19.8% in 2Q26; 13.7% in 3Q25), lower than our estimate 
(16.2%) and the consensus (17.3%). Factoring in the equity investment loss of 
TWD150m+, its 3Q26 EPS was TWD1.06, lower than our forecast (TWD1.68) and the 
consensus estimate (TWD1.65).

We have a Buy (1) rating with a 12-month TP of TWD96, based on a target PER of 17x 
applied on our 1-year-forward EPS forecast. For more information on this company, please 
refer to our latest flash note, 2Q26 review: mainly dragged by Specialized, on 14 August.

What’s the impact 
• 3Q26 results recap. Merida’s 3Q26 revenue of TWD6,098m (-12.1% QoQ, -15.6% 
YoY) missed our and the Bloomberg consensus estimates by 13.6% and 11.1%, 
respectively, which we attributed to the discount when destocking the older models. Its 
gross margin came in at 15.2% (vs. 19.8% in 2Q26; 13.7% in 3Q25), lower than our 
estimate (16.2%) and the consensus (17.3%), due to less OBM sales, the price discount 
for further inventory digestion and the depreciation of the Euro. Factoring in the equity 
investment loss of TWD150m+, its net profit of TWD176m (-44.8% QoQ, -71.9% YoY) 
missed our and the consensus estimates by 65.0% and 65.6%. Its 3Q26 EPS was 
TWD1.06, lower than our forecast (TWD1.68) and the consensus estimate (TWD1.65).  
• 2026 outlook update. Merida revised down its 2026 full-year shipments to 960k units 
(Taiwan plant 280k, down from previous 340k; China plant 680k, down from previous 
700k), as it continues to manage its existing model shipments and expects new models 
in 2027 to bring stronger demand. The company now sees recovery in Europe and 
China, with inventory digestion coming to an end: 9M26 sales in Europe grew 14% YoY, 
driven by strong demand for road bikes and gravel bikes; 9M 26 sales in China grew by 
1% while shipments grew by 7% (vs. revenue guidance of declining 15-20% at the 
beginning of 2026), driven by low- and mid-end models. 
• SBC expected to be profitable in 4Q26. Due to SBC’s conservative tax assumption 
(over 100% effective tax rate) in 1Q-3Q26, if and when the tax rate realised at year-end 
is determined to be less than currently allocated for, we expect there will be a huge profit 
recognised in 4Q26 to offset the estimated after-tax loss recognised in 1Q-3Q26. Merida 
and SBC will review the tax assumption policy after full-year results are out, as the 
policy has caused a great deal of investor confusion. Including USD15m of tariff refund 
in 3Q26, SBC’s pre-tax profit grew by 42% YoY for 9M26. Merida expects there is 
another USD5m of tariff refund to collect in 4Q26.

What we recommend 
We have a Buy (1) rating with a 12-month TP of TWD96, based on a target PER of 17x 
applied on our 1-year-forward EPS forecast. The stock is currently trading at PERs of 
13.2x/10.6x, based on our 2026/27E EPS, vs. its past-5-year one-year-forward PER 
trading range of 7-33x. Key downside risks: slower-than-expected e-bike penetration in the 
EU and the US, worse-than-expected demand in an inflationary environment and inventory 
digestion.

In the interests of timeliness, this document has not been edited.

Update
Realtek Semiconductor  | Asia Pacific
Asia Pacific Summit 2026
Feedback
Realtek remains relatively resilient despite sluggish PC demand, supported by its
diversified product portfolio and platform agnostic strategy. Near-term customer
inventory adjustments appear to reflect shipment timing rather than underlying
demand weakness. Meanwhile, elevated legacy DRAM prices continue to pressure
device BOM costs, potentially delaying broader adoption of edge Al and other

consumer applications
Looking ahead, edge server and edge Al represent structural growth opportunities,
supported by ongoing development in high-speed SerDes and integrated Al
solutions. Realtek also sees opportunities to strengthen its position in higher-end TV
SoCs amid competition. A normalization in memory prices could provide an
additional catalyst for downstream demand recovery.

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

公司簡介 
矽力杰為電源管理晶片大廠，產品線包括
DC/DC 轉換晶片、電池充電管理晶片、過
流保護晶片、靜電防護裝置與 LED 背光驅
動晶片、能源監控管理晶片等。矽力杰
3Q26 產品終端應用主要區分為五大領域，
包括消費電子( 如 平 板 電 腦 、 STB 與
TV)34%、工業應用(如 LED 照明、伺服器
與安防設備等)37%、資訊產品(如筆電)7%
與網路通訊(如路由器、手機等)7%、車載
15%。

主要競爭對手：TI、Maxim、ON Semi、

王文雯 wenwen.wang@sinopac.com 
本次報告：法說會

矽力*-KY (6415 TT) 
明年營收成長性可望達 30%

永豐觀點 
資料中心與車載為成長主軸，兩者總計明年營收比重可望達 45%。 
 投資評價與建議 
維持買進：研究部預估矽力杰 2026 年稅後 EPS 10.92 元(原預估 11.95 元)，2027
年稅後 EPS 15.32 元。考量矽力杰資料中心與車載仍將持續帶動營運拓展，研究部
維持買進建議，目標價維持 644 元(42X 2027 年 EPS 評價)。

ESG 評析 
矽力*-KY 企業永續評鑑整體為 B 等，於 SASB 產業永續議題與跨產業五大永續面
向平均得分分別為 B+等與 B 等。 
SinoPac+ ESG 評鑑系統評等

資料來源：SinoPac+ ESG 評等 (依循 2023 SinoPac+企業永續評鑑方法學) 
註 1：資誠永續發展服務股份有限公司僅於ＥＳＧ評鑑系統方法學建置過程中，就評分指標提供
專業意見，對於評分結果及評估報告內容之完整性及真實性，不負擔保責任，亦不對閱讀或使用
本評估報告之第三方負任何責任。

SinoPac Securities Investment Service licenses and applies the SASB Materiality Map®

### Page 2

2

矽力杰為電源管理晶片大廠：矽力杰成立於 02/2008，為電源管理 IC 設計公司，
產品線包括 DC/DC 轉換晶片、電池充電管理晶片、過流保護晶片、靜電防護裝置
與 LED 背光驅動晶片、能源監控管理晶片等。矽力杰 3Q26 產品終端應用主要區
分為五大領域，包括消費電子(如平板電腦、STB 與 TV)34%、工業應用(如 LED 照
明、伺服器與安防設備等)37%、資訊產品(如筆電)7%與網路通訊(如路由器、手機
等)7%、車載 15%。國際電源管理晶片設計同業與矽力杰產品類似者為 TI、Maxim、
ON Semi 與 ADI，台系類似的參與廠商則包括致新、茂達等。 
3Q26 營業費用較高：矽力杰公布 3Q26 營收 72.59 億元(+21%QoQ，+52%YoY)，
毛利率 47.58%，稅後獲利 9.09 億元(-42%QoQ，+34%YoY)，稅後 EPS 2.31 元，
財報不如預期，主要是營業費用規模偏高。矽力杰 3Q26 財報細項指標如下：(1) 
營收 72.59 億元(+21%QoQ，+52%YoY)，此係來自資料中心訂單加持、車載上
升(新款晶片推出與市占率提升)、消費電子季增 14%(即便市場需求疲弱，但因公
司新款晶片推出，故反而仍能向上)、資通訊產品下滑(因記憶體報價上漲，推高終
端售價而影響消費力道)。(2) 毛利率由 2Q26 的 47.03%微增至 47.42%，符合公
司原預期。(3) 營業費用 24.78 億元，季增達 19%，主要是銷售費用、資料中心應
用研發費用、3Q26 應屆畢業生招募人力增加。(4) 業外損失約 1100 萬元，相較
2Q26 業外收益 9.58 億元出現明顯差異，主要是前期有加特蘭微電子的再評價增
值利益產生。(5) 存貨 70.36 億元，季增 23%，存貨天數 150 天，達到歷史上緣位
置，公司表示係因封測供給吃緊，導致封測交期拉長(部分達到六個月)，因此 wafer bank
囤積在封測廠，導致庫存水位增加。

### Page 3

4Q26 營收持平到小增，略有不如預期：矽力杰預期(1) 4Q26 營收持平到小增(研
究部原預估 QoQ+5%~6%)，其中營運動能仍以資料中心為成長主軸，不過消費
電子市況相當疲弱(公司推測可能與客戶因應原物料與供應鏈不確定性，而提前出
貨，導致 4Q26 拉貨動能驟減)，包括手機與 TV 均顯弱勢。(2) 毛利率隨產品結構
變化、Gen4 製程平台滲透率提升至 20%，使得毛利率應能優於 3Q26 水準。
(3)4Q26~1H27 營業費用建議以 3Q26 的水準計算。研究部預估矽力杰 4Q26 營
收 74.29 億元(+2%QoQ，+38%YoY)，毛利率 47.84%，稅後獲利 10.5 億元
(+16%QoQ，+30%YoY)，稅後 EPS 2.67 元。 
2027 年營收成長性落於 YoY+20%~+30%上緣，反映成長力道延續：矽力杰維持
預期資料中心、車載與為主要成長推手(兩者 2027 年營收總計 YoY+>50%)，並給
予更明確的營收比重目標值：(1) 資料中心(包括主板/企業級 SSD/光模組)目前營
收比重 17%，2027 年底預計可達 25%。公司資料中心三大項目比重依序為光通
訊>SSD>MB，其中光通訊與 SSD 個別均為 mid-to-high single digit，MB 比重
雖較低，不過主板業務(包括控制晶片與 SPS)的新款晶片目前客戶均已處驗證中，
預計 2027 年將放量(公司表是北美客戶的 SPS 需求快於中國)。公司表示儘管近期
資料中心有諸多因素干擾，不過客戶針對明年訂單需求仍相當穩固。(2) 車載營收
比重可望由現階段 15%，攀升至 2027 年的 20%(原預估 15%~20%)，主要動能
來自於 content value 提升(由 200~200USD 往 270~300USD 邁進，係來自 MCU
與高電壓/大電流晶片出貨)、中國電動車的海外銷售攀升。(3) 公司期待隨 Gen4
製程平台 2027 年滲透率達 50%、Gen5 製程平台量產與產品結構持續往資料中
心、車載布局，對 2027 年毛利率>50%有信心。不過研究部認為矽力杰過往毛利
率容易受到存貨損失調整(因庫齡政策)影響，又製造成本上漲壓力延續，故雖預估
2027 年毛利率趨勢向上，但上升的幅度以較保守的態度推估。

維持買進：研究部預估矽力杰 2026 年營收 255.51 億元(+36%YoY)，毛利率
47.6%，稅後獲利 42.98 億元(+72%YoY)，稅後 EPS 10.92 元(原預估 11.95 元)，
並預估 2027 年營收 338.3 億元(+32%YoY)，毛利率 48.98%，稅後獲利 60.32 億
元(+40%YoY)，稅後 EPS 15.32 元。考量矽力杰資料中心與車載仍將持續帶動營
運拓展，研究部維持買進建議，目標價維持 644 元(42X 2027 年 EPS 評價)。

## Document 230: [000660] SK하이닉스(000660) HBM 하나로 시가총액 설명SK하이닉스(000660) HBM 하나로 시가총액 설명HBM 하나로 시가총액 설명 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[000660] SK하이닉스(000660) HBM 하나로 시가총액 설명SK하이닉스(000660) HBM 하나로 시가총액 설명HBM 하나로 시가총액 설명 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-11-04
Pages: 12
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

반도체(Overweight): Stress Test  
90 
SK하이닉스  (000660) 
2026.11.04

HBM 하나로 시가총액 설명 
[반도체/전기전자] 고의영 
2122-9179 ey.ko@imfnsec.com

업황 우려에 고유 할인까지 겹친 과도한 하락 
투자의견 Buy, 목표주가 230만원으로 커버리지를 개시한다. ‘27년 BPS에 목표 
P/B 2.3배를 적용했다[표1]. 이는 상장 이후 평균 P/B의 +2표준편차에 해당한다. 
‘26~’28년 ROE는 각각 102%, 66%, 47%로, ‘26년 정점 이후에도 과거 업사이클 
고점보다 높은 수준이 유지될 전망이다.  
한편, 이익 지속성에 대한 믿음이 공고해져 시장의 시야가 ‘28년으로 향할 경우 
‘28년 BPS 기준 목표주가는 360만원까지 열릴 수 있다[표2]. 
동사 주가는 ‘26년 6월 고점 대비 10월 저점 기준 57% 하락해 과거 다운사이클 
평균 하락폭인 -51%를 넘어섰고, 과거 8~13개월에 걸쳐 진행됐던 조정이 
이번에는 4개월 만에 나타났다. 시장이 ‘28년 이익 급감을 상정하고 있는 것이다. 
그러나 당사는 아래와 같은 투자포인트와 향후 주가 흐름을 제시한다.

‘28년 HBM 가치, 시가총액의 1.3배 
HBM 비중이 높아질수록 동사의 이익 변동성은 낮아질 것이다. 특히 cHBM으로 
갈수록 고객별 수주생산 제품으로 현물시장과 유통 재고가 없고, 생산에 앞서 
물량과 가격이 결정된다. 동사는 이 전환이 업계에서 가장 선두권이다.  
당사는 동사 HBM 매출이 ‘26년 43조원에서 ‘28년 183조원으로 증가할 것으로 
전망한다. 만약, 동사의 ‘26~’28년 HBM 영업이익에 시기별 TSMC P/E(21배, 
15배, 12배)를 적용하면 HBM 가치는 동사 시가총액의 47%, 93%, 135%를 
설명한다[표3]. ‘27년에 이미 HBM 하나만으로 현재 시가총액이 설명된다는 
의미다. 시장이 통상적으로 받아들이는 멀티플 10배를 타겟팅해도 결과는 크게 
다르지 않다.

150만원에 사면 ‘30년 EPS 2배 
동사의 주주환원은 자사주 매입/소각 중심일 것으로 상정한다. 당사는 동사가 현 
정책 기간(‘25~’27년)에 이어 ‘28년 이후에도 FCF의 50%를 환원하고 이 중 
70%를 자사주 매입/소각에 쓸 것으로 가정했다.  
당사 가정대로라면 총주주환원수익률은 현 시가총액 대비 ‘27년 14%, ‘28~’30년 
연 17~24%다[표4]. 
관건은 매입 단가다. 자사주 매입의 주당가치 개선 효과는 P/E가 낮을수록 
커지는데, 현 주가의 ‘27년 P/E는 2.7배다. 현 주가 수준인 150만원에 매년 균등 
매입한다고 ‘30년까지 주식수는 50% 줄어든다.   
그 결과 ‘30년 주당순이익은 매입이 없을 때 대비 2배가 된다[표4]. ‘28년 이후 
이익이 정체되더라도 주당 이익은 계속 늘어나는 구조이며, 이익 지속성에 대한 
의심이 주가를 누를수록 소각 효과는 커진다. 현 주가 수준의 정체가 장기화되기 
어려운 이유다.

### Page 2

반도체(Overweight): Stress Test  
91

‘27년 주가 경로: 회복, 박스권, 재평가 
연말까지는 급락 이후 저점을 높여가는 회복 구간이다. 솔리다임 상장 철회에 이어 3분기 
실적발표에서 ‘26년 주주환원 100조원과 매 분기 자사주 매입 방침이 제시되면서 환원 
의지는 검증되었다. 
‘27년 상반기까지는 ‘28년 불확실성에 대한 우려가 상존할 전망이다. 가격 하락폭은 LTA 
방어력이 확인되기 전까지 블랙박스다. 이 불확실성이 멀티플을 누르는 동안 ‘27년 이익은 
계속 상향되므로 EPS 상승과 멀티플 하락이 상쇄되는 구간이 이어질 전망이다. 이 구간의 
기회비용은 환원으로 보상받는다. 
‘27년 하반기 이후에는 실적 추정의 기준 연도가 ‘28년으로 넘어가고, 신규 팹 물량이 소량 
출하되면서 센티먼트상 우려는 커질 것이다. 그러나 당사는 2H27에 ‘28년 HBM 협의와 
LTA 연장이 지속적으로 확인되면서 이익이 스트레스 테스트 범위 안에서 유지될 것으로 
전망하며[그림2], 이것이 숫자로 확인되는 시점에 순환 할인이 축소되고 재평가가 나타날 
것으로 판단한다.

업사이드 요인 
① AI 투자 지속성 우려로 최근 주가 하락이 가팔랐던 만큼, 이 부분에 대한 우려가 
완화되면 반등의 속도와 폭이 예상보다 빠를 수 있다 
② 향후 주주환원 규모가 예상보다 더 적극적일 경우 추가적인 업사이드가 될 수 있다. 
혹은 주주환원 이후에도 남는 대규모 FCF의 활용 방안도 촉매가 될 수 있다.

Compliance notice 
당 보고서 공표일 기준으로 해당 기업과 관련하여, 
 회사는 해당 종목을 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 금융투자분석사와 그 배우자는 해당 기업의 주식을 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 당 보고서는 기관투자가 및 제 3자에게 E-mail등을 통하여 사전에 배포된 사실이 없습니다.  
 회사는 6개월간 해당 기업의 유가증권 발행과 관련 주관사로 참여하지 않았습니다. 
 당 보고서에 게재된 내용들은 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
본 분석자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위한 참고자료이며, 따라서, 본 자료에 의한 투자자의 투자결과에 대해 어떠한 목적의 증빙자료로도 사용될 수 없으며, 어떠한 경우에도 작성자 및 
당사의 허가 없이 전재, 복사 또는 대여될 수 없습니다. 무단전재 등으로 인한 분쟁발생시 법적 책임이 있음을 주지하시기 바랍니다.

[투자의견] 
종목추천 투자등급

## Document 244: [005930] 삼성전자(005930) 결국 제자리를 찾아갈 것삼성전자(005930) 결국 제자리를 찾아갈 것결국 제자리를 찾아갈 것 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[005930] 삼성전자(005930) 결국 제자리를 찾아갈 것삼성전자(005930) 결국 제자리를 찾아갈 것결국 제자리를 찾아갈 것 - 기업.pdf

### Page 1

기업분석 2026.10.30 www.daishin.com

 결국 제자리를 찾아갈 것 
류형근

hyungkeun.ryu@daishin.com

투자의견 BUY  
매수, 유지 
6개월 목표주가 500,000 
유지  
현재주가 
(26.10.29) 
231,000 
반도체업종

◼ 3분기 실적발표에 대한 시장의 반응은 다소 냉소적. 
◼ 주주환원은 시점의 문제일 뿐. 강화 방향성은 여전히 유효. 
◼ 메모리반도체 이익의 폭증이 가격의 매력을 유발. 비중확대 전략 유효.

매력적인 가격, 변하지 않은 주주환원 정책의 방향성 
목표주가와 매수의견 유지. 메모리반도체 주도 폭발적 이익 상승이 가격 (주
가)의 매력을 지속 유발하고 있고, 주주환원 정책의 강화 방향성에도 변한 것
은 없다는 판단. 절대적으로 싼 주가를 비중 확대의 기회로 삼을 필요. 
1. 주주환원, 시점의 문제 
금번 실적발표에 대한 시장의 반응은 다소 냉소적. 대외 불확실성이 큰 만큼, 
시장은 자사주 매입/소각과 같은 즉각적 주가 부양 정책의 발표를 기대.  
당사는 삼성전자의 주주환원이 “시점의 문제”라 생각. 총 주주환원의 규모도, 
차기 (2027-2029년) 주주환원 정책의 강화 방향에도 변한 것은 없다는 판
단. 시장이 기대하는 자사주 매입/소각은 우선주 중심으로 시행될 가능성이 
높으며, 시행 시, 그 규모는 25조원 내외일 것 (DPS 12,567원)으로 전망. 
2. 메모리반도체, 더 올라갈 것 
보수적 환율 전망 (2027년 평균 환율 1,300원 적용)에도, 2027년 메모리반도
체 영업이익은 608조원에 달할 것. 이익 전망의 상향 여력 또한 유효. 
1) 최대 생산, 최대 판매 
삼성전자는 금일 4Q26 DRAM 판매량 가이던스로 전분기 대비 10% 중후반 
성장 (q-q +18%로 추정)을 제시. 일부 투자자들은 높아진 기저 부담으로 
2027년 판매 성장이 제한될 것을 우려. 당사는 높아진 기저에도 2027년 
DRAM 판매가 전년대비 20% 상승할 것으로 전망. 
- 극단적 수급 불균형 속, 삼성전자는 “최대 생산, 최대 판매” 정책을 시행 
중. 삼성전자만의 강력한 Execution이 20% 대 성장을 유지시킬 원동력. 
- DRAM 1c 수율의 조기 개선 속, 범용 DRAM 양산에 DRAM 1c Capa가 일
부 투입될 것으로 예상. Bit 성장을 높일 수 있는 변화가 될 것. 
2) 열려 있는 가격의 상방 
1Q27 모바일 DRAM에서 Surprise가 발생할 것이라는 의견을 유지 (전분기 
대비 30% 이상 상승). 장기계약이 체결된 CSP향으로도 가격의 추가 상승 여
력이 존재한다는 점을 상기할 필요. CSP의 경우, 당장의 비용 부담보다 AI 시
장 선점을 우선시. 장기계약으로 형성된 가격 Band의 천장 (Ceiling) 또한 시
장 우려 대비 낮지 않음을 감안할 필요. 2027년 범용 DRAM ASP는 전년대비 
40% 중후반까지 상승할 가능성 (현 전망: 전년대비 34% 상승).  
3) HBM, 제품 경쟁력과 점유율의 동반 성장  
최근 삼성전자향으로 HBM4/HBM4e 추가 공급 요청이 쇄도하고 있는 것으
로 추정. 일부 고객은 연내 추가 공급 시 2027년 계약 가격을 선제 지급할 
의사를 표출. 제품 경쟁력이 회복되었음을 보여주는 증거라 생각. 
제품 경쟁력의 회복과 최대 Capa의 이점 속, 2027년은 HBM 점유율 1위를 
확보하는 기념비적 해가 될 것으로 전망 (매출액 기준 점유율 38% 예상).

www.imeritz.com

두산에너빌리티 (034020)

Company Brief 
유틸리티/건설 
Analyst 문경원, CFA 
kyeongwon.moon@meritz.co.kr 
RA 이동석 
dongseok.lee@meritz.co.kr 3Q26 Review: 바닥을 잡는다면 지금  
믹스 개선에 따른 이익률 개선 조금씩 관찰 중 
3Q26 연결 영업이익은 2,612억원(+90.6% YoY)을 기록하며 컨센서스(2,455억원)
를 소폭 상회했다. 밥캣(1,674억원)이 컨센서스(1,761억원)를 소폭 하회한 가운데, 
에너빌리티 부문 영업이익(+179% YoY, OPM 6.6%)이 호실적을 이끌었다. 환율 하
락에도 불구, 가스터빈, 원전 등 고수익성 프로젝트의 비중 상승이 주요 원인이다. 
3Q26 누적 수주는 10.0조원(+85.5% YoY)을 기록했다. 4Q26 가스복합 EPC 수주 
등을 감안하면 연간 가이던스(13.3조원) 달성은 무난하다. 한수원과의 협상 결렬로 
이목이 집중된 체코 두코바니 시공 수주는 2027년 인식될 확률이 높아 보인다. 주간
사가 바뀌더라도 이미 프로젝트에 참여 중인 동사를 배제하기는 쉽지 않다.

대미투자 이후, 연말~연초 수주 모멘텀에 집중 
연말 연초에 다음과 수주 모멘텀이 집중되면서, 주가 상승을 이끌 전망이다. 
1) 대형원전: 정부 자금 중심의 Westinghouse LLI(장납기 기자재) 발주가 몰린다. 
우선 미국 DOE(에너지부)가 제공한 대출을 기반으로 미국 수주가 기대된다.  폴란드 
정부 역시 최근 LLI 발주 작업을 시작했다. 한편, 한국 정부는 연말까지 100억 달러 
규모의 대미 투자 송금을 협의 중인데, 이 역시 DOC(상무부) 주도의 대형 원전 건설
을 위한 LLI 발주에 쓰일 가능성이 높다 
2) SMR: 오래 기다려왔던 Nuscale 향 수주가 시작되고 있다. 우선 29일 모듈 1기 
제작 계약을 체결했다. 현금이 부족한 Nuscale이 용기를 냈다는 건, 곧 Nuscale-
TVA 간 PPA 가 임박했음을 의미한다. 본격적인 수주는 PPA 직후 예상된다.  
3) 가스 터빈: 2026년 초 해외 수주(7기)는 2029년까지 인도 계획이다. 이후 2030
년 슬랏 예약을 완료하고 2031년 물량을 논의 중으로 보인다. 지금은 2030년 슬랏
에 대한 PO가 나와 수주잔고에 인식할 타이밍이다. 역시 수출 물량으로 추측한다.

성장에 대한 기대가 사라진 밸류에이션, 지금이 바닥 
투자의견 Buy를 유지, 할인율 상승을 반영하여 적정주가를 109,000원으로 -17% 
하향 조정한다. 금리 상승, 환율 하락, 트럼프 지지율 하락 등 Top-down 리스크에 
최근 조정을 받았지만, 추가적인 주가 하락 폭은 제한적일 전망이다. EV/선행 수주잔
고 지표는 현재 1.0배 수준까지 하락하며, 원전의 성장성이 주목받지 못했던 
2021~2024년 수준에 근접하게 내려왔다.  
 Meritz Research  2026. 10. 30

Buy (20거래일 평균종가 대비 상승 여력 기준) 
적정주가 (12개월) 109,000원 
현재주가 (10.29) 56,700원 
상승여력 92.2%

TUẦN 09-13/11 [Điểm nhấn thị trường] 
VNINDEX tăng điểm trong tuần qua, +9.57 điểm tương ứng +0.55%, với thanh 
khoản suy giảm

[Nhận định xu hướng] 
Diễn biến giằng co có thể còn tiếp diễn, tuy nhiên khi xu hướng ngắn hạn vẫn 
đang nghiêng về chiều tăng, chỉ số được kỳ vọng sẽ tiếp tục hướng lên, thử thách 
lại các vùng cản phía trên

### Page 2

2

Điểm nhấn thị trường 
Chỉ số, nhóm ngành, khối ngoại và phái sinh

Top 5 ngành tăng / giảm  VNINDEX tăng điểm trong tuần qua, +9.57 điểm tương ứng +0.55%, với thanh khoản suy 
giảm. Tập đoàn công nghiệp, Tiện ích Điện và Bất động sản là các nhóm ngành có mức tăng 
rõ rệt nhất trong tuần qua. Khối ngoại duy trì bán ròng so với xu hướng của tuần trước với 
giá trị -810 tỷ đồng, tập trung ở các mã FPT, VCB và STB; mặt khác, tự doanh đảo chiều bán 
ròng ở mức -13 tỷ đồng.

Ở một khía cạnh khác, khối ngoại duy trì bán ròng -1,161 HĐTL VN30F1M trong tuần qua 
và đang duy trì vị thế bán lũy kế -12,049 hợp đồng. Tự doanh đảo chiều bán ròng -2,033 
hợp đồng và đang duy trì trạng thái lũy kế bán ròng -6,103 hợp đồng.

TTCK Việt Nam trải qua tuần hồi phục nhẹ với biến động tăng/giảm đan xen, thanh khoản 
ở mức thấp và nhà đầu tư nước ngoài tiếp tục đẩy mạnh bán ròng. Hai phiên giữa tuần 
chứng kiến sự hồi phục mạnh mẽ của thị trường, phản ứng trước thông tin NHNN có động 
thái hỗ trợ thanh khoản hệ thống ngân hàng để hạ lãi suất thông qua nghiệp vụ hoán đổi 
ngoại tệ, và mua vào dự trữ ngoại hối. Dù vậy, áp lực bán nhanh chóng quay trở lại ở 2 
phiên cuối tuần khi nhà đầu tư hoài nghi về triển vọng hạ lãi suất trong ngắn hạn, trong 
khi TTCK toàn cầu cũng có diễn biến kém khả quan trước đà hồi phục của giá dầu, số liệu 
CPI Mỹ cao hơn kỳ vọng, và diễn biến tiêu cực ở nhóm cổ phiếu AI.

Source: Trading View, KB Securities Vietnam

Ngắn hạn 
(Dưới 3 tháng)

Trung hạn (Từ 3 đến 9 tháng)

Chỉ báo  Nhận định xu hướng Hành động 
Xanh đậm  Khả năng tăng điểm cao Mua mạnh 
Xanh nhạt  Khả năng tăng điểm vừa phải Mua từng phần 
Vàng  Xu hướng trung tính Mua/Bán trading 
Cam  Khả năng giảm điểm vừa phải Bán từng phần 
Đỏ  Khả năng giảm điểm cao Bán mạnh

Ngưỡng kỹ thuật

VNINDEX tiếp tục hình thành thêm một mẫu nến spinning trên khung tuần, hàm ý tương 
quan cung cầu đang tạm thời cân bằng; trong khi diễn biến tăng nhẹ về điểm số nhưng đi 
kèm sự sụt giảm của thanh khoản cho thấy tín hiệu hồi phục mang tính phân hóa rõ nét. 
Trên khung ngày, nhịp bật tăng tích cực của chỉ số sau khi chạm đường biên dưới của mẫu 
hình tam giác đi ngang trung hạn đang cho dấu hiệu chững lại tại vùng kháng cự gần 
quanh 1.750, phản ánh lực cầu mặc dù được củng cố khá tốt ở vùng giá thấp nhưng thiếu 
sự đồng thuận khớp lên các vùng giá cao. Diễn biến giằng co có thể còn tiếp diễn, tuy 
nhiên khi xu hướng ngắn hạn vẫn đang nghiêng về chiều tăng, chỉ số được kỳ vọng sẽ tiếp 
tục hướng lên, thử thách lại các vùng cản phía trên. 
(Ghi chú: NĐT có thể dựa theo nhận định xu hướng thị trường để tham khảo khuyến nghị 
hành động tương ứng trong bảng phía trên) 
Kháng cự xa: 1790 (+-15) 
Kháng cự gần: 1750 (+-10) 
Hỗ trợ gần: 1700 (+-10) 
Hỗ trợ xa: 1670 (+-15)

### Page 4

4

Danh mục đầu tư mẫu

Phương pháp tiếp cận: 
- Danh mục tập hợp các cổ phiếu tiềm năng nhất được KBSV 
lựa chọn và tái cơ cấu theo tháng. 
- Ngưỡng cắt lỗ cần được cân nhắc cho mỗi mã là -15%.

trọng số được phân bổ đều giữa các mã.

## Document 332: Cliff Notes_ risks mount (2026-11-26)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Cliff Notes_ risks mount (2026-11-26).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-11-26
Pages: 10
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

#cliffnotes #rba #australia #inflation #us #fomc #ecb #aud #usd
Cliff Notes: risks mount
By Ryan Wells, Elliot Clarke & Illiana Jain
10:31 November 20 2026 Share
Key insights from the week that was.
Australia’s labour market disappointed again in October, employment falling 43.5k following
September’s 36.4k decline. On a three-month average basis, job gains over the year have now slowed
to 1.1%yr, materially below current growth in the working-age population, 1.7%yr. As a result, the

unemployment rate rose to a new post-pandemic high of 4.9% despite the participation rate falling
another 0.2ppts to 66.6%.

### Page 2

While employment growth’s descent initially reflected softer momentum in hiring, now we are seeing
evidence of a material increase in redundancies. Most affected workers look to have remained in
employment to date, but persistent job shedding raises the risk of a faster and more persistent uptrend
in the unemployment rate, potentially above our baseline peak forecast of 5.2% (at mid-2027) and most
certainly before the late-2028 peak of 5.1% the RBA expects.

As such, the robust 1.1% (3.5%yr) rise in the Q3 Wage Price Index warrants close scrutiny. The Q3
increase was skewed towards those on awards and enterprise arrangements – pay outcomes directly
tied to the Fair Work Commission’s decision and collective bargaining. Individual arrangements
meanwhile, the most responsive to current and expected labour market conditions, accounted for their
smallest share of a Q3 wage increase since 2019. Business survey detail also suggests labour cost

growth will normalise quickly after the award increase.
Deteriorating economic conditions are also increasingly evident in housing. The Westpac Housing
Sentiment Index fell 2.2% to 83.2 in November, among the weakest readings since 2005. Interestingly,
there remains considerable latent demand: almost 13% of consumers intend to purchase a home over
the next year, and relatively few plan to sell. For now, though, weak sentiment, high interest rates and

expectations of further price declines are keeping would-be buyers on the sidelines. These factors also
pose risks to the broader growth story.
In the US, abstracting from the Anthropic IPO, the outlook for inflation remained the critical theme. The
October FOMC minutes highlighted that inflation is currently being supported by tariffs and energy
prices as well as the AI buildout, with "several [members] judg[ing] that price pressures were
increasingly extending beyond industries directly affected by data center construction”. Participants

"assessed that medium- and longer-term inflation expectations continued to be consistent with the
Committee's 2 percent objective", although persistent above-target inflation is a threat to the status
quo, and perceived risks are skewed to the upside. FOMC members who spoke throughout the week
also kept the focus on price risks, and called for the FOMC to act swiftly. Governor Waller again
proposed a 50bp hike in December; others seemingly favour a 25bp hike at the December and January

meetings, as Westpac forecasts, and continuing to tighten further in 2027 if necessary.
Very clearly, the labour market and activity growth are not of concern for US policy makers. Retail sales
gave further justification for this view this week, the control group surprising to the upside with a 0.6%
gain in October after a material upward revision to September (0.5%). That said, housing is under
considerable pressure, pending home sales falling 17.1% over the past year, and housing starts back

at 2019 levels despite a 9.9% rebound in October. Persistent weakness in this sector will, in time, have
implications for both consumption and investment.
China’s October data round was the other key international development of the week. Again, it provided
a call for pro-active stimulus in scale. In the month, annual retail sales growth almost stalled at 0.4%yr
and the decline in fixed asset investment broadened outside property, with capital expenditure now

down 8.1% across the economy year-to-date. Industrial production again highlighted that its output to
satiate global demand which is holding up aggregate growth; though, even here, momentum softened
in October, annual growth in production slowing from 5.5%yr to 4.8%yr. It is not only activity that

### Page 3

Related articles
remains under pressure, but also wealth, with new / existing home prices 0.15% / 0.24% lower in the
month. As we continue to emphasise, to prove effective, stimulus needs to affect change in both activity
and wealth and do so in a sustainable manner.

## Document 336: Construction rotates from infrastructure to housing and data centres (2026-11-26)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Construction rotates from infrastructure to housing and data centres (2026-11-26).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-11-26
Pages: 18
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

#australia #construction
Construction rotates from infrastructure to housing and data
centres
By Pat Bustamante
Senior Economist, Westpac
14:36 November 25 2026 Share
Construction activity rose 0.9%qtr in Q3 and 5.8%yr. Construction activity remained solid,
driven by residential building (+3.1%qtr), non-residential building (+1.5%qtr, includes data
centres), partly offset by engineering construction (-0.9%qtr).

### Page 2

Construction activity rose 0.9%qtr in Q3 2026, taking annual growth to a strong 5.8%. The quarterly
increase was close to Westpac Economics’ 0.8%qtr forecast, but well above the 0.4%qtr market
consensus. Importantly, sizeable upward revisions to private construction lifted annual growth by
almost 2ppts. These revisions suggest the economy was somewhat stronger in 2025–26 than
previously thought, despite the same observed inflation outcomes.

What does this mean for the near-term outlook?
Overall, today’s data show construction activity continuing to expand at a solid pace, although there are
important offsetting forces beneath the headline result. Public infrastructure work remains soft and is
expected to ease further as major east-coast projects reach completion. This is being partly offset by
public non-residential construction, particularly health, schools and other social infrastructure.
At the same time, private construction remains strong. Residential construction is up 9.3% over the
year, the commercial building pipeline, which includes data centres, is around record highs, and the
renewable-energy investment pipeline remains substantial as we work towards the 82% renewable
electricity target by 2030.
Taken together, demand in the construction sector is likely to remain elevated in the near term, but its

### Page 3

composition is shifting. The pullback in public infrastructure is freeing up labour and other construction
capacity for housing, data centres, renewables and commercial projects. This rebalancing has
already been substantial, with our estimates suggesting the government footprint in the
construction industry declined by around 1.5ppts in 2025–26 (Government’s footprint plateaus as
services rise and construction retreats).
What does this mean for the National Accounts?

Today’s release is the first of a set of partials which will feed into next week’s September quarter 2026
National Accounts. Today’s numbers alone point to stronger growth in residential and new buildings
activity over the quarter and some upside for our Q3 estimate. However, with several important partial
indicators still to come, we will provide our full assessment in Friday’s GDP preview.
What about inflationary pressures and the impact of the data centre boom?

The spike in construction costs during the pandemic was largely synchronised across dwellings, non-
residential buildings and engineering construction. Today, however, cost pressures differ as demand
profiles across these segments diverge.
While rebounding in the quarter, engineering construction costs have been softer as major public
infrastructure programs wind down. In contrast, dwelling construction costs are accelerating as activity
in this segment picks up. Non-residential building costs appear to be going sideways, rather than

significantly reaccelerating, despite the strength in private investment, including data centres.

### Page 4

At 4.5%yr, aggregate construction cost growth is above what was recorded over 2018-19. This raises
questions about whether the strong demand in pockets like data centre investment are contributing to
broader inflationary pressures. For now, there is limited evidence of this happening. However,
construction cost deflators continue to firm in year-ended terms, suggesting that the disinflation
anticipated by the RBA may take longer to materialise.
How this plays out going forward depends on how much the substantial roll-off in public construction

activity makes room for the pickup in private residential and non-residential building work, particularly
data centres, and in private engineering construction associated with the renewable energy transition.
Total construction work yet to be done has risen to around 9.2% of nominal GDP in Q2 2026, compared
with an average of around 8.8% since 2022.
If that pipeline continues to build faster than public infrastructure releases capacity,
construction-sector cost pressures could prove more persistent than currently anticipated.

However, this is not our central case, as higher interest rates and ongoing cost increases are
likely to dampen private demand, while the public infrastructure pipeline continues to wind
down.

### Page 5

The detail
Residential construction outperformed, rising 3.1%qtr in Q3, following an upwardly revised 2.8%qtr
increase in Q2 2026 (previously estimated at 1.7%qtr). Growth was driven by a 3.9%qtr increase in
private residential construction, its strongest quarterly gain since June 2005, outside the COVID period.
In year-ended terms, residential construction is up 9.3%yr, the strongest growth rate since the
September quarter of 2016. This suggests the sector is responding to growing housing demand by

expanding output despite ongoing cost pressures. Some of this strength may also reflect the pullback
in public construction activity, which is freeing up labour and other resources for residential and non-
residential projects.
The strength in residential construction suggests the sector is responding more meaningfully to
Australia's housing shortage. However, with approvals likely to soften as higher interest rates bite and
demand moderates, the current pace of expansion may prove difficult to sustain.

### Page 6

Engineering construction (EC) declined 0.9%qtr in Q3 2026 but was 1.3%higher in year-ended
terms.
As foreshadowed in this month’s Market Outlook, the public infrastructure pipeline appears to have
passed its peak, as major transport projects across Australia’s east coast, particularly in NSW and
Victoria, move towards completion. Consistent with this, public engineering construction declined
3.0%year in Q3 but stabilised over the quarter to post a gain of 2.9%qtr. More broadly, the trend

remains weak, with activity falling in five of the past seven quarters.

### Page 7

In contrast, private engineering construction remains robust, up almost 5% over the year. However,
activity fell 3.7%qtr in Q3, suggesting some moderation may also be emerging in this segment. One
area of continued strength is electricity infrastructure, with the value of private work on electricity
generation and distribution rising by an estimated 10%qtr in seasonally adjusted and nominal terms,
largely reflecting renewable energy projects such as wind and solar developments.

Looking ahead, the private engineering pipeline remains supported by structural investment themes,
particularly the energy transition and nationwide rollout of data centres. However, constraints around
utility provision, grid connections and supporting infrastructure remain a key risk to the timely delivery
of these projects.
Non-residential building construction increased 1.5%qtr in Q3 2026, leaving activity 10.2% higher
over the year. Private-sector activity was mixed, declining 0.4%qtr, but remained 11% higher in year-

ended terms. Public activity also picked up sharply, rising 6.2%qtr to be 8.3% higher over the year,
partly reflecting investment in health and other facilities required to service a growing population.
Overall, non-residential construction remains strong, supported by two key investment themes: the
rollout of data centres in the private sector and increased public investment in health and other social
infrastructure, including in Queensland.

### Page 8

Although the contribution from individual projects is difficult to quantify, the surge in data centre
commencements during the second half of 2025 is likely supporting activity and should remain an
important source of construction demand over the second half of this year and beyond.

### Page 9

State detail
Construction activity increased across four of the six states in Q3, although outcomes varied
considerably.
South Australia recorded the strongest quarterly increase, rising 3.9%qtr, followed by NSW (+2.2%qtr)
and Victoria (+0.8%qtr). In contrast, activity declined in Western Australia (–1.9%qtr) and Queensland
(–1.2%qtr).
Despite the quarterly volatility, construction activity remained higher than a year ago across all states.
Tasmania recorded the strongest annual growth (+17.3%yr), followed by South Australia (+11.3%yr),

NSW (+8.1%yr), Western Australia (+6.4%yr), Victoria (+4.4%yr) and Queensland (+2.6%yr).

It looks like the sell-oﬀ in risk assets has run its course for now,

investors are starting to carve out some boundaries. Levels below

have to soften its tightening stance – remains to be seen. But at
one point, two-year US swap rates were 12bp lower on the day.
They have since retraced a large chunk of that drop.

more importantly, it has revised up its forecast for Korea's current
account surplus to $500bn this year and $540bn next year. It's
hard to think that Korea's prior record current account surplus was
$123bn in 2025. Strong domestic growth rates mean that these
overseas earnings can come home and that won strength will be
welcomed.

The Korean won was one of our preferred currencies in our 2027 FX
Outlook, and our end-2027 USD/KRW target of 1275 feels a little
conservative.

## Document 384: Is the economy ‘plucked’_ (2026-11-25)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Is the economy ‘plucked’_ (2026-11-25).pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 31
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

A New Keynesian ‘plucking’ model of unemployment and inflation
PRELIMINARY AND INCOMPLETE
Anthony Brassil* and Callum Ryan*
Research Discussion Paper
20XX-YY
December 2025
*Economic Research Department
Reserve Bank of Australia
The views expressed in this paper are those of the authors and should not be attributed to the
Reserve Bank of Australia. Any errors are the sole responsibility of the authors.
Author: ryanca at domain rba.gov.au
External Communications: rbainfo@rba.gov.au

https://doi.org/10.47688/rdp20XX-YY

### Page 2

Abstract
We construct a New Keynesian model with search-and-matching frictions in the labour market that
can generate the nonlinear ‘plucking’ dynamics of unemployment in the data: unemployment tends to
spike upwards and then slowly decline. The model features endogenous layoffs, a ‘slippery’ job ladder,
downward nominal wage rigidity, and convex recruiting costs. It generates both the ‘deepness’ and
‘steepness’ asymmetries in unemployment dynamics, and broadly matches the response of labour-

market flows in a downturn. The model endogeneously generates a Phillips curve relationship that
is strongly nonlinear and state-dependent, and a short-run non-accelerating rate of unemployment
(NAIRU) that fluctuates asymmetrically over the business cycle. We also show that cyclical properties
(e.g. shock volatility, nominal rigidities, policy regime) can have a significant effect on the long-run
NAIRU via agents’ precautionary response to future uncertainty.

JEL Classification Numbers:
Keywords:

### Page 4

Figure 1: Unemployment rate in Australia
*Long-run NAIRU estimate from Ballantyne and Cusbert (2025).
1. Introduction
The unemployment rate in the data has strongly nonlinear dynamic (Figure 1). It spikes sharply in
occasional downturns, then declines slowly but persistently in recoveries.1 It is right-skewed in both
levels (i.e. asymmetric ‘deepness’) and in changes (i.e. asymmetric ‘steepness’) (Sichel 1993). Gross
flows between unemployment and employment display similar nonlinearities. Standard search-and-

matching models of the labour market struggle to generate these nonlinearities in unemployment
and labour-market flows (Ferraro 2023).
If this asymmetric pattern stems from frictions within the labour market, what implication do those
frictions have for the relationship between inflation and unemployment? Research using nonlinear
structural models to explore the asymmetric dynamics in the labour market tend to abstract from price
frictions and inflation dynamics (e.g. Dupraz, Nakamura and Steinsson 2025; Ferraro 2018; Petrosky-

Nadeau, Zhang and Kuehn 2018), while in the New Keynesian literature, the usual approach of
linearising equilibrium conditions excludes these nonlinear dynamics by assumption. The 2020s
inflation surge has reignited research into the nonlinearity of the Phillips curve, and the extent to which
this nonlinearity stems from frictions within the labour market (e.g. Benigno and Eggertsson 2023).
But this literature tends not to model the labour market in as much detail.

In this paper, we explore the extent to which a detailed search-and-matching model of the labour
market with pricing frictions can match the nonlinear dynamics in the labour market. We then use
the model to examine the implications of these nonlinearities for the Phillips curve relationship and
the non-accelerating rate of unemployment (NAIRU).
1 Friedman (1964) uses the ‘plucking’ description for the asymmetric way in which output seems to be occasionally ‘plucked’

below trend, before recovering back to trend.

### Page 5

2
We start with a general-equilibrium search-and-matching model similar to Bloesch, Lee and
Weber (2024), in which firms post wages and vacancies and set prices (subject to nominal rigidities
and recruiting costs), and workers search (while unemployed and on the job) and decide whether to
accept and quit jobs. Policy steers aggregate demand to balance inflation and unemployment targets.
We extend this model to include endogenous layoffs, downward nominal wage rigidity, and a ‘slippery’

job ladder (drawing on Dupraz et al (2025)).2 We find that these features - together with convex
recruiting costs - are collectively key to achieving both the asymmetric spikes in unemployment when
shocks occur and the slow speed in the subsequent recovery. The model is sufficiently detailed to
broadly match the dynamics of unemployment and gross flows, but tractable enough that we can
obtain the global nonlinear solution with rational expectations and aggregate uncertainty. This is

important because agents’ precautionary response to future uncertainty is a significant determinant
of outcomes, especially the long-run NAIRU.
We show that the model can generate substantial asymmetry in both ‘deepness’ and ‘steepness’
(comparable to or greater than in the data). The response of unemployment, job-finding, and
separations after a stylised negative demand shock broadly matches the response of these variables in
downturns over the past 50 years. Aggregate demand shocks have an especially asymmetric effect,

because the effect on inflation amplifies the effect of the downward nominal wage rigidity. When
supply shocks occur, the inflation response somewhat eases the downward nominal wage constraint.
The model endogenously generates a Phillips curve relationship that is strongly nonlinear and state-
dependent. Conditional on some initial state of the economy, the Phillips curve is much steeper for
low rates of unemployment. When the initial unemployment rate is higher, the Phillips curve shifts to

the right, and when the initial level of real wages is higher, it shifts upwards.
We can also use the model to simulate a ‘short-run NAIRU’, which is the unemployment rate consistent
with eliminating inflationary pressure from the labour market at some cyclical horizon (e.g. 1-2 years).
This short-run NAIRU varies asymmetrically with the state of the economy. It tends to rise when
unemployment is high, but also when unemployment has fallen rapidly (because real wages increase).

Finally, we can use the ‘stochastic steady state’ of the model to explore the determinants of the long-
run NAIRU, i.e. the unemployment rate consistent with stable inflation in the absence of shocks (but
where agents still expect that shocks may occur in the future). We find that when firms account for
the interaction between aggregate risk and asymmetric wage adjustment costs in their wage-setting
decisions, it generates a trade-off between unemployment variance and steady-state unemployment.

For example, if policy places greater weight on reducing the gap between unemployment and the
long-run NAIRU (instead of the gap between inflation and target), it tends to increase the long-run
NAIRU. This is because by mitigating fluctuations in labour demand, policy reduces the risk of hitting
downward nominal wage rigidities. Firms are then more willing to set higher real wages in steady
state, which reduces the optimal amount of employment in steady state.

The remainder of this introduction discusses the literature. Section 2 outlines the model. Section 3
evaluates the extent to which it can match the nonlinear dynamics in labour-market data. Section 4
considers the implications for the Phillips curve and NAIRU.
2 By ‘slippery’ job ladder, we mean that workers recently hired out of unemployed are much more likely to be laid off.

### Page 6

3
Literature review. The cyclical asymmetries in unemployment are established empirically by e.g.
Sichel (1993), McKay and Reis (2008), and Dupraz et al (2025). Ferraro (2023) analyses the ability of
the baseline Diamond-Mortensen-Pissarides (DMP) model to produce these asymmetries, and finds
that it cannot generate the ‘steepness’ asymmetry (i.e. right-skewness in unemployment changes).
Petrosky-Nadeau et al (2018) finds that the DMP model can generate the ‘deepness’ asymmetry given

large enough shocks.
Dupraz et al (2025) and Ferraro (2018) propose search-and-matching models that can generate
sharp spikes and slow declines in unemployment. We take several features from Dupraz et al (2025),
including downward nominal wage rigidity and a ‘slippery’ job ladder. Pizzinelli, Theodoridis and
Zanetti (2020) explore the state-dependence of unemployment fluctuations in a model with
endogenous layoffs and on-the-job search.3 Adjemian, Karamé and Langot (2021) estimate a

nonlinear model with endogenous separations, downward wage rigidity, worker heterogeneity and
segmented markets, and show that it can generate similar asymmetries in unemployment, hiring,
and separations to the data.
Our definition of a ‘short-run NAIRU’ is similar to the concept discussed by Hall and Kudlyak (n.d.), who
argue that the natural rate of unemployment may vary cyclically and track the actual unemployment
rate during recoveries. Hall and Kudlyak (2022) discusses the ability of DMP-style models to generate

the slow persistent recovery in unemployment that occurs after recessions. Fujita and Ramey (2007)
show that adding vacancy adjustment costs to a DMP model can produce a more sluggish response
for vacancies and hiring after a downturn, slowing down the recovery in unemployment. Mercan,
Schoefer and Sedláček (2024) suggest a congestion mechanism, whereby new hires and incumbent
workers are imperfect substitutes in production, which also propagates unemployment after a shock.

This literature above generally abstracts from price rigidities and inflation dynamics. There is a
substantial literature incorporating labour-market frictions into New Keynesian models (see e.g.
Blanchard and Galí 2010). Recent examples include Moscarini and Postel-Vinay (2023), Faccini and
Melosi (2025), and Bloesch et al (2024). These papers focus on the role of on-the-job search and wage
competition between firms in inflation dynamics. We adopt a similar model for wage setting, drawing

on the tractable formulation in Bloesch et al (2024). But these papers use linear approximations.
The literature on downward nominal wage rigidity explores the extent to which it generates a
nonlinear Phillips curve and asymmetric cyclical dynamics (see e.g. Kim and Ruge-Murcia 2009;
Abbritti and Fahr 2013; Daly and Hobijn 2014; Benigno and Antonio Ricci 2011; Schmitt-Grohé
and Uribe 2022; Benigno and Eggertsson 2023; Benigno and Eggertsson 2024). Lepetit (2020)

also constructs a New Keynesian model with asymmetric unemployment dynamics, although the
asymmetry stems from the convex relationship between the job-finding rate and unemployment.
This literature does not model the labour market in as much detail (e.g. endogenous layoffs, on-the-
job search, job ladder) and does not seek to match the ‘plucking’ property in unemployment to the
same extent.
3 Fujita and Ramey (2012) also analyse versions of the DMP model with endogenous separations and on-the-job search,

but their focus is not on asymmetries in unemployment rate dynamics

### Page 7

4
2. Model
The model consists of firms, workers, and a policy rule. We start by outlining the firms’ problem
and optimal choices. Firms’ optimal decision-making drives most of the model’s mechanics. We then
discuss workers and the policy regime, which are modelled more simply. 5 contains a full list of
equilibrium conditions, as well as details of the calibration and solution method.
2.1 Firms
Firms make decisions around hiring, separations, and price- and wage-setting.

Hiring: Firms choose how many vacancies to post, V i t . These vacancies generate Mi t = qtV i t matches,
where qt is determined by a matching function and depends on aggregate matching efficiency and
labour market tightness. A proportion 1 − ϕ E t of these matches are unemployed searchers (where ϕ E t
is the share of unemployed searchers in total searchers, weighted by search intensity).
Firms must screen matches. Then the individual productivities of employed matches are revealed.

Firms make offers to those with productivities above some threshold xi t , which is 1−F i t of them (where
F i t = F(xi t ) and F is the CDF of the individual productivity distribution). For unemployed matches,
firms have no information about their individual productivity and make offers to all of them. This
feature proxies for the idea that firms may be more uncertain about the capabilities of unemployed
applicants.
Matches with offers choose whether to accept them; Ace t (W i t ) and employed matches and Acu t (W i t )

of unemployed matches do so, where W i t is the real wage paid by the firm. These job-acceptance
probabilities are characterised in the description of workers below.
The number of new hires is given by
Hi t = ϕ E t Mi t (1 − F i t )Ace t (W i t ) + (1 − ϕ E t )Mi t Acu t (W i t ).
Define Hi,u t = (1 − ϕ E t )Mi t Acu t (W i t ) as the number of hires from unemployment.

Separations: There are two types of separation: quits and layoffs. Each period, Qt (W i t ) of firms’
existing employees quit (either to unemployment or to another job). Just as firms’ hiring rate is

increasing in the real wage they offer (via the job-acceptance probabilities Ace t (W i t ) and Acu t (W i t )),
their quits rate is decreasing in the real wage by the firm. This function is characterised in the section
below on workers.
Workers’ probability of being laid off depends on whether they are insecurely or securely attached.
Insecurely attached employees are all those who have just been hired out of unemployed in
the previous period. Before aggregate shocks are revealed, some portion of new hires from

### Page 8

5
unemployment, δ u, (i.e. who were hired the previous period), draw a new productivity from
distribution G. Those whose productivities are less than some threshold xi,m t−1 are laid off, which
is Gi t−1 of them (where Gi t = G(xi,m t−1) and G is the CDF of the distribution).4 All insecurely attached
employees who are not laid off then because securely attached (together with all those who have
been employed for more than one period).
After aggregate shocks are revealed and quitting decisions made, δ of securely attached employees

draw a new individual productivity. Firms then decide to lay off those whose productivities are less
than some threshold xi t , which is F i t of them (where F i t = F(xi t ) and F is the CDF of the individual
productivity distribution). The layoff probability for securely attached employees will be lower than
for insecurely attached employees both because δ < δ u and the distribution F has less mass at low
values than G.

2.2 Workers
All workers who are unemployed when shocks are revealed search for jobs. A share λ EE of employed
workers also search. The total number of searchers is then λ EE ˜Nt−1 + 1 − ˜Nt−1, and the effective
share of employed searchers is
ϕ E t = λ EE ˜Nt−1
λ EE ˜Nt−1 + 1 − ˜Nt−1 .
Those who enter unemployment after shocks are revealed, either because they are laid off or because

they quit to unemployment, do not search in that period.6
A share λ EU of employed workers consider quitting to unemployment.
6 This means that even if hiring costs went to zero, unemployment would remain above zero. If the newly unemployed
were allowed to search, this would raise the average number of searchers, especially in periods where layoffs are high.
Workers hired last period who are laid off before shocks are revealed can search. This is to avoid Hu t−1 or Nt−1 being a

state variable.

Workers’ only role in the model is to determine the labour supply schedule faced by firms. They do so
by determining the hiring and quits elasticities ε He t , ε Hu t and ε Q t via the functions Ace t (Wt ), Acu t (Wt )
and Q(Wt ).

wage differential. These preferences are drawn from a Gumbel distribution with parameter γ. If a

Monetary policy: Policy tries to implement a flexible inflation target, but is subject to various shocks
(e.g. it cannot fully offset all demand shocks immediately). Therefore:
ln Πt − ln Πtarget + γ p ( Nt − Ntarget ) = ln Mt

where Mt is an exogenous nominal demand shock.
2.4 Equilibrium, solution and calibration
We assume that firms have rational expectations, which implies that they understand the interaction
between nonlinearities in the economy and aggregate risk when forming their expectations. This
means, for example, that in the presence of downward nominal wage rigidity, firms will set lower
wages even in steady state, given the risk that it will be very costly for them to reduce wages if

negative shocks occur in the future.
We compute the global solution to the nonlinear model, so that we can explore how the interaction
between nonlinearities and aggregate risk affects expectations and dynamics. The state variables are
employment ˜Nt−1 (after insecurely attached employees are laid off), real wages Wt−1, the average
idiosyncratic productivity of employees ¯xt−1, and the aggregate productivity and demand shocks, At
and Mt .
The model is calibrated so that steady-state values for unemployment and key labour-market flows

are broadly similar to their values Australian data around 2025, as well as to match the standard
deviation of the unemployment rate over 1980 to 2019 and generate a similar positive skewness of
unemployment rate changes.
See Appendix 5 for more detail on the calibration and solution method.
3. How well can we match the dynamics of the labour market in the data?
3.1 Comparing the model to labour-market dynamics in the data
In this section, we compare the dynamics of unemployment and labour-market flows in the model

to Australian data. Figure 2 illustrates the path of transition rates and flows between labour-market
states in Australia since 1980. It shows that the UE rate is negative correlated with unemployment,
even though UE flows (as a proportion of the labour force) are positively correlated. UE flows increase
when the pool of unemployed is larger, but this increase is not enough for the UE rate to increase.

### Page 14

11
Figure 2: Labour-market transition rates and flows in Australia
Note: The UE and EU flows in the data are divided by the labour force size to make them comparable to
the model flows. The shaded regions represent contractions, which are identified using the algorithm in
Dupraz et al (2025).
Figure 3: Comparing recessions in the model and the data
Note: The UE and EU flows in the data are divided by the labour force size to make them comparable to the model

flows.
The figure also shows that the EU rate and EU flows are postively correlated with unemployment,
and the EE rate (i.e. job-to-job transition rate) is negatively correlated (at a cyclical frequency).
Figure 3 compares a stylised recession in our model to recessions in the data since 1980.9 The model
downturn is generated with a sequence of six demand shocks (over six months) calibrated to produce
a similar path for unemployment to the downturns in the data. The paths for transition rates and

flows from the model are broadly similar to the data. In particular, the UE rate falls while UE flows
increase, and the magnitude of all responses is comparable to the data.
9 We exclude the 2010-14 and 2022-25 upswings because they involved a gradual increase in unemployment rather than
a spike.

### Page 15

12
Next, we consider whether our model can reproduce the ‘plucking’ or asymmetric ‘sharp spike’ and
‘slow decline’ patterns we see in the unemployment rate data. One measure of this pattern is the
skewness of unemployment rate changes. In the data, this moment is quite positive, reflecting
frequent small decreases and occasional large increases (Table 1) (Sichel (1993) called this a
‘steepness’ asymmetry). Standard search-and-matching models tend to generate negative skewness

in unemployment changes (Ferraro 2023). In contrast, under the current calibration, simulations of
our model generate a substantially positive skewness of unemployment rate changes, although not
quite as large as in the data.
We can also compare other moments of the unemployment rate and moments of the unemployment-
to-employment (UE) and employment-to-unemployment (EU) transition rates in our model to the
data. The model generates somewhat higher variance and negative skewness in the UE rate, whereas

the UE rate is positively skewed in Australian data over this sample period. In US data, the UE rate
has higher variance and is negatively skewed (see Appendix A.3). The model matches the variance
and skewness of the EU rate well, but generates more skewness in changes in the EU rate than in
the data.

Skewness 1.12 0.59 -0.38 0.40 0.52 0.57
Skewness of changes 1.37 1.71 -0.74 -0.62 2.02 0.69
Notes: These moments use Australian data from 1980 to 2019, detrended using estimates from Ballantyne and Cusbert (2025). See
Appendix A.3 for moments using undetrended data and using US data.
3.2 Explaining the model’s dynamics
The IRFs in Figure 4 demonstrate why we can match these moments. The responses are
strongly nonlinear. Large negative shocks generate a large increase in the unemployment rate

(disproportionately more than small negative shocks), whereas positive shocks do not reduce it very
much. Unemployment recovers very slowly after an initial spike.
To explore why the model is able to generate these asymmetric dynamics, Table 2 compares the
model’s moments when three key frictions are removed, either one at a time or all together. First, the
downward nominal wage rigidity is removed by making wage adjustment costs symmetric. The next

column removes insecurely attached workers, so that all workers have the same layoff rate. The next
column removes convexity in recruiting costs, making them (almost) linear. The final column removes
all three frictions. In each case, we recalibrate the model so that it generates the same steady-state
unemployment rate and flows, and the same variance in unemployment. Figure 5 compares the
response of each version of the model to positive and negative demand shocks.

When all three frictions are removed (the last column in Table 2), the degree of asymmetry
declines and the skewness of unemployment rate changes is much lower. Unlike the standard DMP
model (Ferraro 2023), the skewness of unemployment changes remains positive, likely because
layoffs are still endogenous, which generates some asymmetry. Figure 5 shows that unemployment
recovers more rapidly, especially the UE rate. The value of a match is a forward-looking object,

determined largely by expected future surplus, and therefore rises quickly after the shock peaks.

### Page 16

13
Figure 4: Response to aggregate demand shocks
Note: The economy is initially at steady state.
When unemployment is high, the vacancy-filling rate is also high, so marginal recruiting costs fall.
Unemployed searchers make up a much larger proportion of total searchers, so for a given amount
of hiring, hires out of unemployment are higher.
